# The SDL3 port, Vulkan first: implementation plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** Move StreamFlex from SDL2, SDL2_image and SDL2_ttf to SDL3, SDL3_image and SDL3_ttf, ask for Vulkan first, recover a lost GPU device, and take the spec's five additions, shipping alone as v0.5.0.

**Architecture:** This plan is written in two stages, as the spec requires (§ 3, § 8). **Phase 0** runs two gates before any port task is written: the Transparent spike, a throwaway SDL3 program that applies StreamFlex's chroma key under each renderer, and the timing measurement, a throwaway SDL2 patch on master that logs where icon and background loading spends its time. **Phase 1 onward** (the port itself) is written only after both gates have reported to the user, because a failed spike or a tripped threshold changes what those tasks build. Nothing from Phase 0 is merged.

**Tech Stack:** C (MSVC `/W4 /WX` on Windows, gcc on Linux), SDL2 2.0.18+ (Phase 0's timing patch), SDL3 3.4.12 from vcpkg (Phase 0's spike), CMake, vcpkg at `9e593bb`, Docker (the headless harness image), PowerShell 7, ImageMagick, GitHub Actions (`workflow_dispatch`).

**Spec:** `design/specs/2026-10-06-sdl3-port-design.md` (PR #42, `dd30307`). Read it in full before any task; this plan argues from it. Research behind it: `C:/Users/jscha/ClaudeScratch/streamflex-sdl3-research/`.

## Global Constraints

- **Version floors** (spec § 2): SDL3 **3.2.10**, SDL3_image **3.2.4**, SDL3_ttf **3.2.2**. Debian and the Pi build at the floor; Arch and Windows at the newest. Code that compiles only on 3.4 fails CI.
- **Renderer preference lists** (spec § 3): Windows `"vulkan,direct3d12,direct3d11,opengl"`, Linux and Pi `"vulkan,opengl"`. When `SDL_RENDER_DRIVER` is set, pass no list.
- **Platforms** (spec, Decisions): Debian trixie, Raspberry Pi OS trixie, Ubuntu 25.10, Arch, Windows, or newer. v0.4.1 is the last release for Debian bookworm and Ubuntu 24.04 LTS. No backport.
- **Timing thresholds, RATIFIED by the user 2026-10-10** (spec § 8): parallel decode is built only if the default menu's `Timing: menu` line exceeds **250 ms**, or `Timing: startup` exceeds **500 ms**, or any menu open or Back exceeds **50 ms** (`SLOW_KEY_MS`).
- **The gates are split (user ruling, 2026-10-10, amends spec § 3 and § 8):**
  - qa-harness runs the timing patch on its **Windows and Ubuntu** guests, with the bundle this plan builds, plus a spike **pre-screen** on its Windows guest (WARP, no GPU).
  - Only the **real-GPU** spike runs on the user's Windows machine. Claude builds it and writes the run sheet; **the user runs it**. Claude never launches the spike or StreamFlex on the user's desktop.
  - qa-harness is asked to slot the timing runs ahead of StreamFlex's place in its queue.
- **Nothing in Phase 0 merges.** The timing patch lives on `spike/sdl2-timing`, pushed only so CI builds its packages, and deleted after the gates. The spike lives in `C:/Users/jscha/ClaudeScratch/streamflex-sdl3-gates/spike/`, outside the repo.
- **Smoke rows with every change** (todo Binding decision): the port's plan carries a smoke-rows task; the spec (§ 9, "Smoke rows") lists each addition's claims. The `smoke-coverage` check is required.
- **Pads for the battery hands-on rows** (user, 2026-10-10): Xbox (wireless and Bluetooth), DualSense / DualShock 4, Switch Pro.
- **Release notes:** the SDL2 notice already opens CHANGELOG `[Unreleased]`; v0.5.0 and the next two releases open with it.
- American spelling in every added line, file name and commit message (gray, color, canceled).
- One implementer per checkout. Commits by pathspec, never `git add -A`. Every git command uses `git -C C:/Users/jscha/source/repos/streamflex`. Branches come from `pwsh C:/Users/jscha/.claude/scripts/git-new-branch.ps1`.
- The build's ledger is `C:/Users/jscha/source/repos/streamflex/.superpowers/sdd/2026-10-10-sdl3-port/progress.md` (git-ignored). Every gate result goes into it verbatim.

## Review Focus

1. **A renderer that cannot be created when forced** (`SDL_RENDER_DRIVER=vulkan` on a machine or guest with no Vulkan driver). Expect: the spike logs `Renderer: none (...)` with SDL's reason and exits 2, and the run sheet records the row as *unavailable*, never as a Transparent pass or fail. Pinned in Task 0.2 (the spike's exit 2 and the run sheet's branch on it).
2. **A see-through fullscreen window the person cannot find to close.** Expect: Esc quits, and the spike quits by itself after 60 s whatever the screen shows. Pinned in Task 0.2 (the 60 s quit, checked in Step 4's `--drivers` dry run and stated in the run sheet).
3. **The OpenGL renderer replacing the native window** (spec § 3: it calls `SDL_RecreateWindow` when the window lacks `SDL_WINDOW_OPENGL`). Expect: the spike reads the HWND after the renderer exists and again at every switch, and logs it, so a stale HWND cannot make OpenGL look broken. Pinned in Task 0.2 (`window_hwnd()` at each switch, the HWND in the `Transparent:` line).
4. **An icon file that exists but is not an image,** passed through the patched loader. Expect: the same `Could not load image <path>` error StreamFlex logs today, no crash, and the menu still draws. Pinned in Task 0.3 (the bundle's `broken.png` entry) and checked in Task 0.1's dry run.
5. **A log overwritten by the next run** (Windows opens `streamflex.log` with `wb`, Linux likewise without `--restarted`). Expect: every run's log is kept under its own name before the next run starts. Pinned in Task 0.3's run instructions and Task 0.2's run sheet (each copies the log after its run).

- [ ] **Step 2: Add the counters and the timed loader to `src/image.c`**

After `NSVGrasterizer *rasterizer = NULL;` (`image.c:37`) add:

```c
// Timing patch (throwaway, never merged): what icon loading spent, summed by render_buttons
Uint64 timing_read = 0, timing_decode = 0, timing_texture = 0;

// Timing patch: whole milliseconds from performance-counter counts
unsigned timing_ms(Uint64 counts)
{
    Uint64 frequency = SDL_GetPerformanceFrequency();
    return (unsigned) ((counts * 1000 + frequency / 2) / frequency);
}

// Timing patch: IMG_Load in two timed parts, the read and the decode, the way IMG_Load does it
// (the file's extension is the type hint)
static SDL_Surface *timed_load(const char *path, Uint64 *read, Uint64 *decode)
{
    Uint64 start = SDL_GetPerformanceCounter();
    size_t size = 0;
    void *data = SDL_LoadFile(path, &size);
    Uint64 loaded = SDL_GetPerformanceCounter();
    *read += loaded - start;
    if (data == NULL)
        return NULL;
    const char *dot = SDL_strrchr(path, '.');
    SDL_Surface *surface = IMG_LoadTyped_RW(SDL_RWFromConstMem(data, (int) size), 1, dot != NULL ? dot + 1 : NULL);
    SDL_free(data);
    *decode += SDL_GetPerformanceCounter() - loaded;
    return surface;
}
```

In `src/image.h`, after the `load_icon` declaration (`image.h:39`), add:

```c
// Timing patch (throwaway, never merged)
extern Uint64 timing_read, timing_decode, timing_texture;
unsigned timing_ms(Uint64 counts);
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Time the background image** — replace the body of `load_texture_measured` from `SDL_Surface *surface = IMG_Load(path);` (`image.c:218`) to its `return load_texture(surface);` (`:225`) with:

```c
    Uint64 start = SDL_GetPerformanceCounter(), read = 0, decode = 0;
    SDL_Surface *surface = timed_load(path, &read, &decode);
    if (surface == NULL) {
        log_error("Could not load image %s\n%s", path, IMG_GetError());
        return NULL;
    }
    Uint64 decoded = SDL_GetPerformanceCounter();
    if (luminance != NULL)
        *luminance = surface_luminance(surface);
    Uint64 lit = SDL_GetPerformanceCounter();
    SDL_Texture *texture = load_texture(surface);
    Uint64 end = SDL_GetPerformanceCounter();
    log_debug("Timing: background: image %s in %u ms (read %u, decode %u, luminance %u, texture %u)", path,
        timing_ms(end - start), timing_ms(read), timing_ms(decode), timing_ms(lit - decoded), timing_ms(end - lit));
    return texture;
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Time the icons**

In `load_icon`, replace from `SDL_Surface *surface = IMG_Load(path);` (`image.c:392`) to `return load_texture(keep_surface_off_chroma_key(surface, path));` (`:397`) with:

```c
    SDL_Surface *surface = timed_load(path, &timing_read, &timing_decode);
    if (surface == NULL) {
        log_error("Could not load image %s\n%s", path, IMG_GetError());
        return NULL;
    }
    // The chroma pass counts as decode: a decode worker would run it too (spec section 8)
    Uint64 start = SDL_GetPerformanceCounter();
    surface = keep_surface_off_chroma_key(surface, path);
    Uint64 kept = SDL_GetPerformanceCounter();
    timing_decode += kept - start;
    SDL_Texture *texture = load_texture(surface);
    timing_texture += SDL_GetPerformanceCounter() - kept;
    return texture;
```

In `rasterize_svg_from_file`, replace `char *buffer = SDL_LoadFile(path, NULL);` (`image.c:368`) with:

```c
    Uint64 start = SDL_GetPerformanceCounter();
    char *buffer = SDL_LoadFile(path, NULL);
    timing_read += SDL_GetPerformanceCounter() - start;
```

In `rasterize`, add `Uint64 start = SDL_GetPerformanceCounter();` as the first statement before `image = nsvgParse(buffer, "px", 96.0f);` (`image.c:297`); after `keep_off_chroma_key(pixel_buffer, width, height, pitch, what);` (`:335`) add `Uint64 rasterized = SDL_GetPerformanceCounter();` and `timing_decode += rasterized - start;`; after `SDL_Texture *texture = SDL_CreateTextureFromSurface(renderer, surface);` (`:343`) add `timing_texture += SDL_GetPerformanceCounter() - rasterized;`. (The highlight and the scroll arrows also pass through `rasterize`; `render_buttons` zeroes the counters at its start, so only its own icons are counted.)

- [ ] **Step 5: Log a menu's render, every open and Back, the slideshow and startup in `src/launcher.c`**

Replace `render_buttons` (`launcher.c:1230-1269`) with:

```c
static void render_buttons(Menu *menu, const LayoutGeometry *geometry)
{
    Uint64 timing_start = SDL_GetPerformanceCounter();
    Uint64 titles_time = 0;
    int icons = 0, titles = 0;
    timing_read = timing_decode = timing_texture = 0;
    int size = geometry->button;
    title_info.font = fixed_title_font;
    title_info.font_size = (int) config.title_font_size;
    if (geometry->title_size > 0) {
        TTF_Font *font = title_font(geometry->title_size);
        if (font != NULL) {
            title_info.font = font;
            title_info.font_size = geometry->title_size;
        }
    }
    int line_height = TTF_FontHeight(title_info.font);
    title_info.max_width = layout_title_width(config.title_shadows, size, line_height);   // Room for the shadow
    titles_time += SDL_GetPerformanceCounter() - timing_start;   // Opening the title font counts as titles
    for (unsigned int i = 0; i < menu->num_entries; i++) {
        Entry *entry = menu->items[i];
        if (entry->icon != NULL)
            SDL_DestroyTexture(entry->icon);
        if (entry->icon_selected != NULL)
            SDL_DestroyTexture(entry->icon_selected);
        entry->icon = load_icon(entry->icon_path, size);
        entry->icon_selected = entry->icon_selected_path != NULL ? load_icon(entry->icon_selected_path, size) : NULL;
        icons += (entry->icon_path != NULL) + (entry->icon_selected_path != NULL);
        if (config.titles_enabled) {
            Uint64 title_start = SDL_GetPerformanceCounter();
            int h;
            if (entry->title_texture != NULL)
                SDL_DestroyTexture(entry->title_texture);
            entry->title_texture = render_text_texture(entry->title, &title_info, &entry->text_rect, &h);
            entry->title_offset = (config.title_oversize_mode == OVERSIZE_SHRINK && h != line_height)
                                  ? (line_height - h) / 2 : 0;
            if (config.title_oversize_mode != OVERSIZE_NONE && entry->text_rect.w > size)
                log_debug("Menu '%s': the title '%s' is %i px wide, over its %i px button",
                    menu->name, entry->title, entry->text_rect.w, size);
            titles_time += SDL_GetPerformanceCounter() - title_start;
            titles++;
        }
    }

    // Keep no pointer to a cached font: another menu's title size may close it
    title_info.font = fixed_title_font;
    title_info.font_size = (int) config.title_font_size;
    log_debug("Menu '%s': rendered its buttons at %i px", menu->name, size);
    Uint64 icons_time = SDL_GetPerformanceCounter() - timing_start - titles_time;
    log_debug("Timing: menu '%s': %i icons at %i px in %u ms (read %u, decode %u, texture %u), %i titles in %u ms",
        menu->name, icons, size, timing_ms(icons_time), timing_ms(timing_read), timing_ms(timing_decode),
        timing_ms(timing_texture), titles, timing_ms(titles_time));
}
```

In `load_menu` (`launcher.c:1087`), add `Uint64 timing_start = SDL_GetPerformanceCounter();` as the first statement, and replace its final `return 0;` (`:1110`) with:

- [ ] **Step 7: Dry-run it in the headless image** (needs Task 0.3's bundle: build the bundle first if this task runs alone)

Create `C:/Users/jscha/ClaudeScratch/streamflex-sdl3-gates/dry-run.sh` with LF line endings:

```bash
#!/bin/bash
# Timing patch dry run: a plain build, Xvfb, the bundle's heavy configs with the keys qa-harness
# will press, and the Timing lines out of each log. Runs in the streamflex-test image with the repo
# at /src (read-only), the bundle at /tmp/sf-timing (read-only) and an output folder at /out.
set -u
rm -rf /work; mkdir -p /work
tar -C /src --exclude=./build --exclude=./.git --exclude=./.superpowers -cf - . | tar -C /work -xf -
cmake -S /work -B /work/build -DCMAKE_BUILD_TYPE=Release > /out/configure.log 2>&1 || { echo "CONFIGURE FAILED"; exit 2; }
cmake --build /work/build -j"$(nproc)" > /out/build.log 2>&1 || { echo "BUILD FAILED"; tail -30 /out/build.log; exit 2; }
Xvfb :99 -screen 0 1920x1080x24 -ac > /dev/null 2>&1 &
export DISPLAY=:99
for i in $(seq 100); do xdotool getdisplaygeometry > /dev/null 2>&1 && break; sleep 0.2; done
log=$HOME/.local/share/streamflex/streamflex.log
for cfg in heavy-image heavy-slideshow; do
    rm -f "$log"
    /work/build/streamflex -d -c "/tmp/sf-timing/linux-$cfg.ini" > "/out/$cfg.out" 2>&1 &
    pid=$!
    sleep 5; xdotool key Return; sleep 2; xdotool key BackSpace; sleep 2
    xdotool key Return; sleep 2; xdotool key BackSpace; sleep 2; xdotool key Escape
    for i in $(seq 50); do kill -0 "$pid" 2> /dev/null || break; sleep 0.2; done
    kill -KILL "$pid" 2> /dev/null; wait "$pid"
    cp "$log" "/out/$cfg.log"
    echo "== $cfg"; grep -E 'Timing: |Could not load image' "/out/$cfg.log"
done
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Write `main.c`**

```c
// StreamFlex SDL3 Transparent spike: throwaway, never merged (spec section 3). It opens a fullscreen
// window the way StreamFlex does (no graphics flag), creates a renderer from StreamFlex's Windows
// preference list, or from SDL_RENDER_DRIVER when that is set, and draws the default chroma key
// (#010101) with a gray square in the middle and a red bar along the bottom. Transparent is
// StreamFlex's own call: WS_EX_LAYERED plus SetLayeredWindowAttributes(..., LWA_COLORKEY), applied
// after the renderer exists, as reload_background() applies it.
//   spike.exe           Transparent from the start
//   spike.exe --live    opaque (dark blue) for 3 s, then Transparent switches on, as from settings
//   spike.exe --drivers print the render drivers this SDL has and quit, with no window
// T toggles Transparent, Esc quits, and it quits by itself after 60 s.
#define WIN32_LEAN_AND_MEAN
#include <windows.h>
#include <stdarg.h>
#include <stdbool.h>
#include <stdio.h>
#include <string.h>
#include <SDL3/SDL.h>
#include <SDL3/SDL_main.h>

#define KEY_R 0x01
#define KEY_G 0x01
#define KEY_B 0x01
#define PREFERENCE "vulkan,direct3d12,direct3d11,opengl"
#define QUIT_AFTER_MS 60000
#define LIVE_SWITCH_MS 3000
#define PROBE_AFTER_MS 2000

static FILE *log_file = NULL;

static void say(const char *format, ...)
{
    char line[1024];
    va_list args;
    va_start(args, format);
    vsnprintf(line, sizeof(line), format, args);
    va_end(args);
    printf("%s\n", line);
    fflush(stdout);
    if (log_file != NULL) {
        fprintf(log_file, "%s\n", line);
        fflush(log_file);
    }
}

// Read the HWND now: the OpenGL renderer may have replaced the native window (spec section 3)
static HWND window_hwnd(SDL_Window *window)
{
    return (HWND) SDL_GetPointerProperty(SDL_GetWindowProperties(window), SDL_PROP_WINDOW_WIN32_HWND_POINTER, NULL);
}

static void set_transparent(HWND hwnd, bool on)
{
    LONG style = GetWindowLong(hwnd, GWL_EXSTYLE);
    if (on) {
        SetWindowLong(hwnd, GWL_EXSTYLE, style | WS_EX_LAYERED);
        if (!SetLayeredWindowAttributes(hwnd, RGB(KEY_R, KEY_G, KEY_B), 0, LWA_COLORKEY))
            say("SetLayeredWindowAttributes failed: error %lu", GetLastError());
    }
    else
        SetWindowLong(hwnd, GWL_EXSTYLE, style & ~WS_EX_LAYERED);
    say("Transparent: %s (HWND %p)", on ? "on" : "off", (void *) hwnd);
}

// Read two pixels of the screen as the desktop composes it: a corner, where the key is drawn, and
// the center, inside the gray square. A see-through corner shows whatever is behind the window.
// A helper only: what the person sees decides.
static void probe(void)
{
    HDC screen = GetDC(NULL);
    HDC memory = CreateCompatibleDC(screen);
    int width = GetSystemMetrics(SM_CXSCREEN), height = GetSystemMetrics(SM_CYSCREEN);
    HBITMAP bitmap = CreateCompatibleBitmap(screen, width, height);
    HGDIOBJ previous = SelectObject(memory, bitmap);
    BitBlt(memory, 0, 0, width, height, screen, 0, 0, SRCCOPY | CAPTUREBLT);
    COLORREF corner = GetPixel(memory, 40, 40);
    COLORREF center = GetPixel(memory, width / 2, height / 2);
    SelectObject(memory, previous);
    DeleteObject(bitmap);
    DeleteDC(memory);
    ReleaseDC(NULL, screen);
    bool keyed = GetRValue(corner) == KEY_R && GetGValue(corner) == KEY_G && GetBValue(corner) == KEY_B;
    bool solid = GetRValue(center) == 0x80 && GetGValue(center) == 0x80 && GetBValue(center) == 0x80;
    say("Probe: corner #%02X%02X%02X (%s), center #%02X%02X%02X (%s)",
        GetRValue(corner), GetGValue(corner), GetBValue(corner), keyed ? "the key color" : "see-through",
        GetRValue(center), GetGValue(center), GetBValue(center), solid ? "solid" : "NOT the square");
}

static void draw(SDL_Renderer *renderer, bool transparent, int width, int height)
{
    if (transparent)
        SDL_SetRenderDrawColor(renderer, KEY_R, KEY_G, KEY_B, 255);
    else
        SDL_SetRenderDrawColor(renderer, 0x1E, 0x2A, 0x44, 255);
    SDL_RenderClear(renderer);
    SDL_FRect square = { (float) (width / 2 - 200), (float) (height / 2 - 200), 400.0f, 400.0f };
    SDL_SetRenderDrawColor(renderer, 0x80, 0x80, 0x80, 255);
    SDL_RenderFillRect(renderer, &square);
    SDL_FRect bar = { 0.0f, (float) (height - 60), (float) width, 60.0f };
    SDL_SetRenderDrawColor(renderer, 0xC0, 0x00, 0x00, 255);
    SDL_RenderFillRect(renderer, &bar);
    SDL_RenderPresent(renderer);
}

int main(int argc, char *argv[])
{
    bool live = argc > 1 && strcmp(argv[1], "--live") == 0;
    bool drivers_only = argc > 1 && strcmp(argv[1], "--drivers") == 0;
    char path[MAX_PATH];
    snprintf(path, sizeof(path), "%sspike.log", SDL_GetBasePath());
    log_file = fopen(path, "w");

    SDL_SetAppMetadata("StreamFlex SDL3 spike", "0", "streamflex-spike");
    if (!SDL_Init(SDL_INIT_VIDEO)) {
        say("SDL_Init failed: %s", SDL_GetError());
        return 3;
    }
    int version = SDL_GetVersion();
    say("SDL %d.%d.%d", SDL_VERSIONNUM_MAJOR(version), SDL_VERSIONNUM_MINOR(version), SDL_VERSIONNUM_MICRO(version));
    for (int i = 0; i < SDL_GetNumRenderDrivers(); i++)
        say("Render driver available: %s", SDL_GetRenderDriver(i));
    if (drivers_only) {
        SDL_Quit();
        return 0;
    }

    SDL_Window *window = SDL_CreateWindow("StreamFlex SDL3 spike", 1280, 720, SDL_WINDOW_FULLSCREEN);
    if (window == NULL) {
        say("Window: none: %s", SDL_GetError());
        SDL_Quit();
        return 3;
    }
    const char *forced = SDL_getenv("SDL_RENDER_DRIVER");
    SDL_Renderer *renderer = SDL_CreateRenderer(window, forced != NULL ? NULL : PREFERENCE);
    if (renderer == NULL) {
        say("Renderer: none (%s): %s", forced != NULL ? forced : PREFERENCE, SDL_GetError());
        SDL_DestroyWindow(window);
        SDL_Quit();
        return 2;
    }
    say("Renderer: %s%s", SDL_GetRendererName(renderer), forced != NULL ? " (from SDL_RENDER_DRIVER)" : "");
    SDL_SetRenderVSync(renderer, 1);
    int width = 0, height = 0;
    SDL_GetWindowSizeInPixels(window, &width, &height);
    say("Screen: %dx%d px, pixel density %.2f, display scale %.2f", width, height,
        SDL_GetWindowPixelDensity(window), SDL_GetWindowDisplayScale(window));
    say("Mode: %s", live ? "live (opaque now, Transparent in 3 s)" : "startup (Transparent from the start)");

    bool transparent = !live, switched_live = !live, probed = false, running = true;
    if (transparent)
        set_transparent(window_hwnd(window), true);
    Uint64 start = SDL_GetTicks(), changed = start;
    while (running) {
        SDL_Event event;
        while (SDL_PollEvent(&event)) {
            if (event.type == SDL_EVENT_QUIT || (event.type == SDL_EVENT_KEY_DOWN && event.key.key == SDLK_ESCAPE))
                running = false;
            else if (event.type == SDL_EVENT_KEY_DOWN && event.key.key == SDLK_T && !event.key.repeat) {
                transparent = !transparent;
                set_transparent(window_hwnd(window), transparent);
                changed = SDL_GetTicks();
                probed = false;
            }
            else if (event.type == SDL_EVENT_WINDOW_FOCUS_GAINED) {
                say("Focus: gained");
                changed = SDL_GetTicks();
                probed = false;
            }
            else if (event.type == SDL_EVENT_WINDOW_FOCUS_LOST)
                say("Focus: lost");
            else if (event.type == SDL_EVENT_RENDER_TARGETS_RESET || event.type == SDL_EVENT_RENDER_DEVICE_RESET ||
                     event.type == SDL_EVENT_RENDER_DEVICE_LOST)
                say("Render event: 0x%X", (unsigned) event.type);
        }
        Uint64 now = SDL_GetTicks();
        if (!switched_live && now - start >= LIVE_SWITCH_MS) {
            switched_live = true;
            transparent = true;
            set_transparent(window_hwnd(window), true);
            changed = now;
            probed = false;
        }
        if (transparent && !probed && now - changed >= PROBE_AFTER_MS) {
            probe();
            probed = true;
        }
        if (now - start >= QUIT_AFTER_MS) {
            say("Quit: 60 s passed");
            running = false;
        }
        if (running)
            draw(renderer, transparent, width, height);
    }
    say("Quit");
    SDL_DestroyRenderer(renderer);
    SDL_DestroyWindow(window);
    SDL_Quit();
    if (log_file != NULL)
        fclose(log_file);
    return 0;
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Write the user's run sheet, `run-spike.ps1`**

```powershell
#Requires -Version 7.2
# The SDL3 Transparent spike's run sheet (spec section 3): eight short runs, four renderers by two
# modes. Each opens a fullscreen window; you look, answer three questions, and the answers and the
# logs go into spike-results.md beside this script. Paste that file back to Claude.
$ErrorActionPreference = 'Stop'
# spike.exe sits beside this script in the zip, and under build/Release in the build folder
$dir = if (Test-Path (Join-Path $PSScriptRoot 'spike.exe')) { $PSScriptRoot } else { Join-Path $PSScriptRoot 'build/Release' }
$exe = Join-Path $dir 'spike.exe'
$logOut = Join-Path $dir 'spike.log'
$results = Join-Path $PSScriptRoot 'spike-results.md'
$logs = Join-Path $PSScriptRoot 'run-logs'
New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force $logs | Out-Null

function Ask([string] $question) {
    do { $answer = (Read-Host "$question (y/n)").Trim().ToLowerInvariant() } until ($answer -in 'y', 'n')
    $answer
}

$gpu = Read-Host 'Which GPU is your main monitor plugged into (for example RTX 3060)?'
$rows = [System.Collections.Generic.List[string]]::new()
$screen = ''
foreach ($driver in 'vulkan', 'direct3d12', 'direct3d11', 'opengl') {
    foreach ($mode in 'startup', 'live') {
        Write-Host ''
        Write-Host "=== $driver, $mode ===" -ForegroundColor Cyan
        Write-Host 'The screen goes fullscreen: a gray square in the middle and a red bar along the bottom.'
        if ($mode -eq 'live') { Write-Host 'It starts dark blue; after 3 seconds Transparent switches on by itself.' }
        Write-Host 'Look: is your desktop visible everywhere except the square and the bar?'
        Write-Host 'Then Alt+Tab to another window and back once, and look again.'
        Write-Host 'Press Esc when done. It also closes by itself after 60 seconds.'
        Read-Host 'Press Enter to start' | Out-Null
        $arguments = @()
        if ($mode -eq 'live') { $arguments += '--live' }
        $env:SDL_RENDER_DRIVER = $driver
        try { & $exe @arguments; $code = $LASTEXITCODE } finally { Remove-Item Env:SDL_RENDER_DRIVER -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue }
        $kept = Join-Path $logs "spike-$driver-$mode.log"
        Copy-Item $logOut $kept -Force
        $log = Get-Content $kept
        $chosen = ($log | Select-String '^Renderer: ' | Select-Object -First 1).Line
        $probes = (@($log | Select-String '^Probe: ' | ForEach-Object Line) -join ' / ')
        if (-not $screen) { $screen = ($log | Select-String '^Screen: ' | Select-Object -First 1).Line }
        if ($code -eq 2) {
            Write-Host "This renderer could not be created: $chosen" -ForegroundColor Yellow
            $rows.Add("| $driver | $mode | unavailable | - | - | $chosen | - |")
            continue
        }
        $through = Ask 'Was your desktop visible through the background?'
        $solid = Ask 'Were the square and the bar solid (not see-through)?'
        $alttab = Ask 'Was it still right after Alt+Tab and back?'
        $note = Read-Host 'Anything else (flicker, a black screen, a frame)? Press Enter for none'
        $rows.Add("| $driver | $mode | $through | $solid | $alttab | $chosen; $probes | $note |")
    }
}

$windows = (Get-CimInstance Win32_OperatingSystem)
@(
    '# SDL3 Transparent spike: results'
    ''
    "- GPU driving the monitor: $gpu"
    "- $screen"
    "- Windows: $($windows.Caption) $($windows.Version)"
    "- Run: $(Get-Date -Format 'yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm')"
    ''
    '| Renderer | Mode | Desktop through | Square and bar solid | After Alt+Tab | Log | Note |'
    '|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|'
    $rows
) | Set-Content -Encoding utf8 $results
Write-Host ''
Write-Host "Done. Paste $results back to Claude (the logs are in $logs)." -ForegroundColor Green
```

- [ ] **Step 6: Write qa-harness's unattended pre-screen, `run-spike-guest.ps1`**

```powershell
#Requires -Version 7.2
# The spike's pre-screen for the qa-harness Windows guest (no GPU, so WARP or nothing): each of the
# eight runs is left for 12 s, then ended, and its log kept. The Probe lines are the result; a
# screenshot at 8 s, if the harness takes one, is better still.
$ErrorActionPreference = 'Stop'
# spike.exe sits beside this script in the zip, and under build/Release in the build folder
$dir = if (Test-Path (Join-Path $PSScriptRoot 'spike.exe')) { $PSScriptRoot } else { Join-Path $PSScriptRoot 'build/Release' }
$exe = Join-Path $dir 'spike.exe'
$logOut = Join-Path $dir 'spike.log'
$out = Join-Path $PSScriptRoot 'guest-results'
New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force $out | Out-Null
& $exe --drivers | Out-Null
Copy-Item $logOut (Join-Path $out 'drivers.log') -Force
foreach ($driver in 'vulkan', 'direct3d12', 'direct3d11', 'opengl') {
    foreach ($mode in 'startup', 'live') {
        $env:SDL_RENDER_DRIVER = $driver
        # -ArgumentList refuses an empty array, so it is passed only for the live mode
        $start = @{ FilePath = $exe; PassThru = $true }
        if ($mode -eq 'live') { $start.ArgumentList = '--live' }
        $process = Start-Process @start
        Remove-Item Env:SDL_RENDER_DRIVER
        if (-not $process.WaitForExit(12000)) { Stop-Process -Id $process.Id -Force; $process.WaitForExit() }
        Copy-Item $logOut (Join-Path $out "spike-$driver-$mode.log") -Force
        "$driver $mode exit $($process.ExitCode)" | Add-Content (Join-Path $out 'exits.txt')
    }
}
Get-ChildItem $out
```

- [ ] **Step 1: Write `make-bundle.ps1`**

```powershell
#Requires -Version 7.2
# Builds the timing gate's bundle (plan Task 0.3): a 4K background, a three-image slideshow, a file
# that is not an image, and heavy configs for Windows (C:\sf-timing) and Linux (/tmp/sf-timing).
$ErrorActionPreference = 'Stop'
# A literal path: the session's shell-write guard refuses a write target it cannot see in the text
$root = 'C:/Users/jscha/ClaudeScratch/streamflex-sdl3-gates/sf-timing'
Remove-Item -Recurse -Force $root -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force (Join-Path $root 'images/slides') | Out-Null

# Plasma noise decodes like a photograph, not like a flat test card; the seeds keep it repeatable
magick -seed 1 -size 3840x2160 plasma:fractal -quality 90 (Join-Path $root 'images/bg-4k.jpg')
foreach ($n in 1..3) {
    magick -seed (10 + $n) -size 3840x2160 plasma:fractal -quality 90 (Join-Path $root "images/slides/slide-$n.jpg")
}
Set-Content -NoNewline -Encoding ascii (Join-Path $root 'broken.png') 'this file is not an image'

$brands = 'netflix', 'prime-video', 'disney-plus', 'hbo-max', 'hulu', 'apple-tv', 'paramount-plus', 'peacock',
          'crunchyroll', 'youtube', 'youtube-tv', 'pluto-tv', 'tubi', 'twitch', 'plex', 'jellyfin', 'emby', 'kodi',
          'vlc', 'audiobookshelf'
$generic = 'movies', 'news', 'kids', 'sports'

function Write-Config([string] $file, [string] $base, [string] $sep, [string] $background) {
    $lines = [System.Collections.Generic.List[string]]::new()
    $lines.AddRange([string[]] @('[General]', 'DefaultMenu=Main', '', '[Background]'))
    $lines.AddRange([string[]] $background.Split("`n"))
    $lines.AddRange([string[]] @('', '[Hotkeys]', 'Hotkey1=#1B;:quit', '', '[Main]', 'Entry1=Big;apps;:submenu Big'))
    $n = 2
    foreach ($icon in $brands + $generic[0..1]) { $lines.Add("Entry$n=$icon;$icon;:quit"); $n++ }
    $lines.Add("Entry$n=Broken;$base${sep}broken.png;:quit")
    $lines.AddRange([string[]] @('', '[Big]'))
    $n = 1
    foreach ($round in 1, 2) {
        foreach ($icon in $brands + $generic) { $lines.Add("Entry$n=$icon $round;$icon;:quit"); $n++ }
    }
    $lines | Set-Content -Encoding utf8NoBOM (Join-Path $root $file)
}

foreach ($platform in @(@{ Name = 'win'; Base = 'C:\sf-timing'; Sep = '\' }, @{ Name = 'linux'; Base = '/tmp/sf-timing'; Sep = '/' })) {
    $b = $platform.Base; $s = $platform.Sep
    Write-Config "$($platform.Name)-heavy-image.ini" $b $s "Mode=Image`nImage=$b${s}images${s}bg-4k.jpg"
    Write-Config "$($platform.Name)-heavy-slideshow.ini" $b $s "Mode=Slideshow`nSlideshowDirectory=$b${s}images${s}slides"
}
Get-ChildItem -Recurse $root | Select-Object FullName, Length
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write `sf-timing/README.md`, the guests' run instructions**

````markdown
# StreamFlex timing gate: what to run (SDL3 plan, Phase 0)

Thank you for slotting this in. Two parts: the timing runs on BOTH guests, and the spike pre-screen on
the Windows guest only. Please send back every file under `results/` (and `guest-results/` for the
spike), whole: the logs, not a summary. Restore each guest's StreamFlex pin (v0.4.1) afterward.

The build is master plus a throwaway patch that only adds `Timing:` debug lines (branch
`spike/sdl2-timing`, CI run <run-id>). It is never released.

## Keys for a "keys" run

Wait until the menu shows, then 5 s; then Enter (opens Big), 2 s, Backspace (back to Main), 2 s,
Enter, 2 s, Backspace, 2 s, Esc (quits).

## Windows guest

1. Unzip the Windows build to `C:\sf-timing\app\` and this bundle to `C:\sf-timing\`.
2. Three runs of each, copying `C:\sf-timing\app\streamflex.log` to `C:\sf-timing\results\<name>-<n>.log`
   after EACH run (the next run overwrites it):
   - `default`: `C:\sf-timing\app\streamflex.exe -d` (the shipped config beside the exe); wait 5 s after the menu shows, Esc.
   - `heavy-image`: `streamflex.exe -d -c C:\sf-timing\win-heavy-image.ini`, the keys run.
   - `heavy-slideshow`: `streamflex.exe -d -c C:\sf-timing\win-heavy-slideshow.ini`, the keys run.
3. Spike pre-screen: unzip `spike.zip` to `C:\sf-timing\spike\` and run
   `pwsh -File C:\sf-timing\spike\run-spike-guest.ps1`. It needs no input (eight runs of 12 s). A screenshot
   of each run at about 8 s helps if your harness can take one.

## Ubuntu guest

1. `sudo apt install ./streamflex_<version>_amd64.deb` (replaces the pinned v0.4.1 for now). Unpack this
   bundle to `/tmp/sf-timing/`.
2. Move any `~/.config/streamflex/` aside first, so `default` runs the shipped config.
3. Three runs of each, copying `~/.local/share/streamflex/streamflex.log` to `/tmp/sf-timing/results/<name>-<n>.log`
   after EACH run:
   - `default`: `streamflex -d`; wait 5 s after the menu shows, Esc.
   - `heavy-image`: `streamflex -d -c /tmp/sf-timing/linux-heavy-image.ini`, the keys run.
   - `heavy-slideshow`: `streamflex -d -c /tmp/sf-timing/linux-heavy-slideshow.ini`, the keys run.
4. Put `~/.config/streamflex/` back and reinstall v0.4.1.
````

Replace `<run-id>` with Task 0.1's run id before zipping.

### Task 0.5: Read the gates and report to the user

**Files:**
- Modify: the ledger (`progress.md`)
- Modify: this plan (`design/plans/2026-10-10-sdl3-port.md`): the Phase 0 results summary and, once the user has ruled, Phase 1 onward

- [ ] **Step 1: The spike verdict.** For each renderer, Transparent *works* only when, in both modes, the user answered y to all three questions. The Probe lines support the answers but never overrule them. A renderer that was *unavailable* on the user's machine is recorded as such, with the guest's pre-screen row beside it. **All four work:** nothing changes (spec § 3). **Any fails:** the measured table goes to the user with the options before Phase 1 is written. Changing renderer on the fly is the user's decision, not a ruling.

- [ ] **Step 2: The timing verdict.** Tabulate per guest, per config, the three runs' figures. Against the ratified thresholds:
  1. the default menu's `Timing: menu 'Main'` line (the `default` config) above 250 ms, or `Timing: startup: first menu shown` above 500 ms;
  2. any `Timing: menu '<name>' opened in` line above 50 ms.

  **Which configs count (user ruling, 2026-10-10):** threshold 1 on `default`; threshold 2 on `default` and on the 24-icon `Main` of both heavy configs (its startup open and every Back to it). Every `Big` (48-icon) line is reported for information only and never trips the gate. If neither threshold trips, parallel decode is not built (spec § 8) and the port's `Timing:` lines ship as written. If one trips, Phase 1 carries the `src/iconload.c` tasks of spec § 8.

- [ ] **Step 3: Report to the user** with both tables and the verdicts, then clean up: delete `spike/sdl2-timing` on the remote and locally (`git -C C:/Users/jscha/source/repos/streamflex push origin --delete spike/sdl2-timing`, then `branch -D`), and keep `C:/Users/jscha/ClaudeScratch/streamflex-sdl3-gates/` until v0.5.0 ships.

- [ ] **Step 4: Write Phase 1 onward** into this plan, from the spec and the gate results, then review, commit, and open the plan's PR.

## Phase 0 results

### The Transparent spike (user's runs 1-5, 2026-10-10; RTX 3060, 3840x2160 at 175%)

| Renderer | Chroma key (`LWA_COLORKEY`) | Per-pixel alpha, fullscreen | Per-pixel alpha, not promotable |
|---|---|---|---|
| direct3d (D3D9) | works | fails | - |
| direct3d11 | fails | works | - |
| direct3d12 | fails | refused by SDL | - |
| opengl | fails | fails | works |
| vulkan, stock SDL 3.4.12 | fails | fails (black) | - |
| vulkan, SDL patched | - | fails | works (centered window; one row short with the taskbar marked; one row taller) |

- **The chroma key fails** under every SDL3 renderer but D3D9: SDL3's D3D11 and D3D12 present with flip-model swapchains, which a layered window's color key never sees.
- **Per-pixel alpha** is `SDL_WINDOW_TRANSPARENT` (DWM blur-behind, `video/windows/SDL_windowswindow.c:759-776`). D3D12 refuses it (`SDL_render_d3d12.c:3499-3502`); D3D11 switches to `DXGI_SWAP_EFFECT_DISCARD` for it (`SDL_render_d3d11.c:887-891`). SDL 3.4.12's Vulkan renderer sets `compositeAlpha` to 0 for it (`SDL_render_vulkan.c:2297`), which is not a valid mode, though this NVIDIA driver offers `PRE_MULTIPLIED` (`vulkaninfo`).
- **Independent flip hid the rest:** a window exactly covering the display skips DWM's composition, so its alpha never reached the screen while a GDI capture still showed the desktop through it. A window one row short or one row taller is not promoted.

**Decision (user, 2026-10-10):** Transparent on Windows uses per-pixel alpha on the list `"vulkan,direct3d11"`. Choosing Transparent recreates the window with `SDL_WINDOW_TRANSPARENT`, one pixel row short of the display and marked fullscreen with `ITaskbarList2::MarkFullscreenWindow`, so the taskbar goes behind it. Every other background keeps true fullscreen on the Vulkan-first list. The SDL3 Vulkan renderer fix (choose `PRE_MULTIPLIED` when `supportedCompositeAlpha` offers it, and fail otherwise so the list falls to direct3d11) rides in the Windows vcpkg overlay port beside the SDL3_ttf fix, and is reported upstream as an issue (SDL forbids AI-written code, so any PR is the user's). The spec is amended before Phase 1 is written (§ 3 Transparent and device loss, Decisions, Out of scope, § 9).

### The timing measurement (qa-harness, 2026-10-11; 18 runs, Windows and Ubuntu guests)

The whole logs are in `C:/Users/jscha/ClaudeScratch/streamflex-sdl3-gates/timing-results-2026-10-11/`, tabulated in the ledger. Renderers: SDL2's `direct3d` on the Windows guest, `opengl` (x11) on the Ubuntu guest. Every run quit on Esc. The box was held quiet throughout.

| Figure (ms, over 3 runs per config) | Windows guest | Ubuntu guest |
|---|---|---|
| `default`: Main, 4 icons | 9-11 | 8-11 |
| `default`: first menu shown | 26-83 | 44-49 |
| `default`: Main opened | 12-14 | 10-13 |
| heavy: Main, 24 icons (read / decode / texture) | 58-79 (3-6 / 41-54 / 13-18) | 38-48 (0-1 / 36-44 / 1-3) |
| **heavy: Main opened at startup** | **61-84** | **41-52** |
| heavy: Back to Main | 0-2 | 0 |
| heavy: Big, 48 icons (information only) | 114-132 | 89-95 |

**Verdict.** Threshold 1 does not trip. **Threshold 2 trips:** the heavy configs' 24-icon Main opens in over 50 ms at startup on 6 of 6 Windows runs and 1 of 6 Ubuntu runs, and decode is most of it. Phase 1 therefore carries parallel icon decode (Task 1.13). `spike/sdl2-timing` was deleted on 2026-10-11.

## Phase 1: the port

**Drafted while the timing gate ran (user, 2026-10-11), then completed by its verdict:** threshold 2 tripped, so Task 1.13 builds parallel icon decode. Nothing else in Phase 1 depends on the timing numbers. Several tasks were split while drafting (1.2a/b, 1.5a/b, 1.6a/b, 1.7a/b, 1.8a/b, 1.9a/b, 1.11a/b, 1.14a/b, 1.16a/b); a split's halves run in order.

**User rulings made while drafting (2026-10-11):** Nintendo pads take SDL3's positional buttons in v0.5.0 ("ButtonA" is the south button on a Switch Pro; the label-matching fix is todo item 26, and the v0.5.0 notes say so); X11 keeps SDL3's default keycode options on non-Latin layouts; the existing NULL `exe_path` crash in `find_default_font()` is fixed in Task 1.2a; the parallel decode is one synchronous batch, its stale-result test replaced by the leak passes' proof; the user has an Xbox Wireless Adapter, no real pad for a Linux guest, and one display (the second-display rows stay manual).

**One branch, `feat/sdl3-port`, cut from master by `git-new-branch.ps1`.** Tasks run in the order below; each one ends green on the build legs it names, with the full headless set through `tests/headless/run-shards.ps1` from Task 1.2 on. A task's implementer sees only that task, the Global Constraints, and the **Interfaces** blocks; the names below are the contract between tasks and are not renamed by any one of them.

### Interfaces between tasks

These are fixed here, so tasks drafted and built apart agree. A task that needs a change to one of them stops and reports; it never renames one alone.

**Task 1.5 produces, in `src/winplan.c` / `src/winplan.h` (pure, no SDL, built into CTest like `layout.c`):**
```c
typedef enum { WINDOW_FULLSCREEN, WINDOW_TRANSPARENT_SHORT } WindowKind;

typedef struct {
    WindowKind kind;
    const char *render_list;   // NULL: pass no list (SDL_RENDER_DRIVER chose)
    bool mark_fullscreen;      // ITaskbarList2::MarkFullscreenWindow on the HWND
    bool refused;              // An override outside the transparent list: use the opaque window
    char reason[128];          // Set when refused: "SDL_RENDER_DRIVER=<name> is not in the transparent list"
} WindowPlan;

// background: the ModeBackground wanted; windows: built for Windows; render_driver_env: getenv("SDL_RENDER_DRIVER"), NULL or "" for unset
WindowPlan window_plan(int background, bool windows, const char *render_driver_env);
```
`#define RENDER_LIST_WINDOWS "vulkan,direct3d12,direct3d11,opengl"`, `RENDER_LIST_UNIX "vulkan,opengl"`, `RENDER_LIST_TRANSPARENT "vulkan,direct3d11"`.

**Task 1.5 produces, in `launcher.c`:**
```c
// The renderer half of create_window(): the list from window_plan() (or none under SDL_RENDER_DRIVER),
// SDL_CreateRenderer, the blend mode, the draw color, VSync and its check, the Video: and Renderer: lines.
// false, with SDL's reason logged, when no renderer could be made; the caller decides what that means.
static bool create_renderer(const WindowPlan *plan);
```

**Task 1.6 produces, in `launcher.c` (declared in `launcher.h`):**
```c
typedef enum { RELOAD_NONE = 0, RELOAD_TARGETS = 1, RELOAD_DEVICE = 2, RELOAD_SIZE = 4, RELOAD_RECREATE = 8 } ReloadReason;
void reload_mark(ReloadReason why);   // Event handlers only mark; nothing reloads inside the drain
void reload_run_pending(void);        // After the drain, before drawing: geometry first, then the textures
bool app_in_front(void);              // state.application_launching || state.application_running
```
and in `src/derive.c` (pure): `ScreenGeometry geometry_from_pixels(int width, int height)` returning `screen_width`, `screen_height`, `screen_margin`, `title_min_size` with today's arithmetic. In `settings_screen.c`: `void settings_refresh_all(void)` (every group in `REFRESH_ORDER`, public) and `void settings_reflow(void)` (fonts, text cache, preview, layout; page and edits kept; an open picker or browser closes as Back closes it).

**Task 1.7 produces, in `src/platform/win32.c` (declared in `platform.h`, Windows only):**
```c
void platform_window_changed(SDL_Window *old_window, SDL_Window *new_window);   // UnregisterHotKey on the old HWND, every hotkey on the new one, and the taskbar mark when the new window is Transparent
```
and in `launcher.c` the device-loss state, `renderer_lost()` and the retry schedule (`RENDERER_RETRIES 5`, `RENDERER_RETRY_MS 2000`).

**Task 1.8 produces, in `launcher.c`:** `static bool recreate_window(const WindowPlan *plan)` (create the new window and renderer first, then move every holder of the window pointer and ID, call `platform_window_changed()`, destroy the old, take the foreground, mark `RELOAD_RECREATE`), and `static void fall_back_to_opaque(const char *reason)`. In `win32.c`: `void taskbar_mark_fullscreen(SDL_Window *window, bool mark)` with the COM setup of § 3.

**Task 1.11 produces** `src/padpower.c` / `.h`: `PadPowerLevel padpower_level(PadPowerState state, int percent, PadPowerLevel shown)` and `int padpower_pick(const PadPower *pads, int count)`.

**Task 1.14 produces** `src/fontset.c` / `.h`:
```c
typedef struct FontSet FontSet;
FontSet *fontset_open(const char *path, float size, int face, const char *role);   // role: "titles", "settings rows", "settings headers", "settings notes"
void fontset_prepare(FontSet *set, const char *text);   // Attach a provider for each character the font lacks, before the first measure
TTF_Font *fontset_font(FontSet *set);                    // The primary, fallbacks attached
bool fontset_resize(FontSet *set, float size);          // Fallbacks first, then the primary
void fontset_close(FontSet *set);                       // The font first, then the fallbacks (#549)
void fontset_quit(void);                                // Before TTF_Quit: the candidate list, FcFini()
```
**Task 1.15 produces** the candidate source behind `fontset_prepare()`: `const FontCandidate *fontset_candidates(int *count)`, built once, logged once.

### What each task must carry

Every task's steps are in the plan's usual form: the failing test first where a test can fail, the code, the run that proves it, the commit by pathspec. Each task also carries:
- its **smoke-row claims** where it changes something a person sees (Task 1.17 writes them; the task names them);
- its **mutants** for every branch its tests leave unexercised, with an unmutated control (spec § 9);
- the **full headless set** after its last edit (Task 1.2 on), and the Windows CI build for any task touching Windows code.

In `vcpkg-overlays/sdl3/portfile.cmake`, insert after the `vcpkg_from_github(...)` call (after its line 9, the closing `)`), with one blank line before and after:

```cmake
# StreamFlex: a transparent window's Vulkan swapchain asks for premultiplied alpha when the surface
# offers it, and fails otherwise, so the renderer is not made and the list moves on to direct3d11
# (StreamFlex spec section 2). SDL 3.4.18 asks for compositeAlpha 0, which is not a valid mode.
# PRE_MULTIPLIED alone: StreamFlex's output over a cleared transparent target is premultiplied, and
# INHERIT would never fall back. Reported upstream as an issue; this block and test 13 go together
# when an SDL release fixes it. vcpkg_replace_string only warns when nothing matches, so a lost fix
# still builds: test 13 (tests/check_vulkan_fix.cmake) is what catches it.
vcpkg_replace_string("${SOURCE_PATH}/src/render/vulkan/SDL_render_vulkan.c"
    [=[swapchainCreateInfo.compositeAlpha = (renderer->window->flags & SDL_WINDOW_TRANSPARENT) ? (VkCompositeAlphaFlagBitsKHR)0 : VK_COMPOSITE_ALPHA_OPAQUE_BIT_KHR;]=]
    [=[swapchainCreateInfo.compositeAlpha = VK_COMPOSITE_ALPHA_OPAQUE_BIT_KHR;
    if (renderer->window->flags & SDL_WINDOW_TRANSPARENT) {
        if (!(rendererData->surfaceCapabilities.supportedCompositeAlpha & VK_COMPOSITE_ALPHA_PRE_MULTIPLIED_BIT_KHR)) {
            SDL_SetError("The vulkan renderer: this surface offers no composite alpha mode for a transparent window");
            return VK_ERROR_FEATURE_NOT_PRESENT;
        }
        swapchainCreateInfo.compositeAlpha = VK_COMPOSITE_ALPHA_PRE_MULTIPLIED_BIT_KHR;
    }]=]
)
```

(The spike's measured replacement, `spike/overlay-ports/sdl3/portfile.cmake:13-29`, with its `POST_MULTIPLIED` and `INHERIT` branches removed. `rendererData->surfaceCapabilities` is filled at 3.4.18's `SDL_render_vulkan.c:2089` before this line, and the function, `VULKAN_CreateSwapChain` at :2086, returns `VkResult`.) Leave `vcpkg-overlays/sdl3/vcpkg.json` as copied: its `"version": "3.4.18"` pins SDL.

Create `tests/test_harfbuzz.c`:

```c
// The built SDL_ttf shapes text with HarfBuzz (spec section 9, test 5). Without it a Windows build
// (sdl3-ttf without [harfbuzz]) draws fallback Arabic as isolated letters in the wrong order, and
// TTF_GetHarfBuzzVersion() reports 0.0.0 (SDL_ttf.c's TTF_GetHarfBuzzVersion, 3.2.2).
#include <SDL3_ttf/SDL_ttf.h>
#include "check.h"

int main(void)
{
    int major = 0, minor = 0, patch = 0;
    TTF_GetHarfBuzzVersion(&major, &minor, &patch);
    printf("HarfBuzz %d.%d.%d\n", major, minor, patch);
    CHECK(major > 0);
    return check_report();
}
```

Create `tests/check_vulkan_fix.cmake`:

```cmake
# The built Windows exe carries SDL3's Vulkan fix for a transparent window (spec section 9, test 13):
# its error string is in vcpkg-overlays/sdl3's patched SDL_render_vulkan.c and in no SDL release. No
# CI runner has a GPU to drive the fix itself, so the string is the proof the overlay port was built.
# Run as cmake -DEXE=<file> -P check_vulkan_fix.cmake
if (NOT EXISTS "${EXE}")
  message(FATAL_ERROR "There is no file at ${EXE}")
endif ()
file(STRINGS "${EXE}" found LIMIT_COUNT 1
     REGEX "this surface offers no composite alpha mode for a transparent window")
if (NOT found)
  message(FATAL_ERROR "${EXE} lacks the Vulkan fix's error string: the sdl3 overlay port was not built")
endif ()
message(STATUS "${EXE} carries the Vulkan fix: ${found}")
```

```dockerfile
# The StreamFlex headless test image: CI's Debian build dependencies (SDL3 at the floor, Debian trixie),
# plus a virtual X display (Xvfb) with Mesa's software drivers, so the Vulkan renderer runs on lavapipe
# and OpenGL on softpipe without a screen; xdotool to press keys, xwd (x11-apps) to take the screenshots
# pixels.py reads, and xrandr (x11-xserver-utils) to read the display's refresh rate. The Noto CJK and
# color emoji fonts and fontconfig's development files serve the fallback-font checks, and Weston (14,
# which has no fifo-v1) the Wayland leg. The launcher runs as `tester`, because root ignores file
# permissions. See run.sh for how it is used.
# Through mirror.gcr.io, Google's public Docker Hub mirror: Docker Hub rate-limits the unauthenticated
# pulls of GitHub's runners. The digest is the same image's.
FROM mirror.gcr.io/library/debian:trixie@sha256:<the digest just printed>
RUN apt-get update \
 && apt-get install -y --no-install-recommends \
      build-essential file git cmake pkg-config \
      libsdl3-dev libsdl3-image-dev libsdl3-ttf-dev libfontconfig-dev libinih-dev \
      xvfb xauth xdotool x11-apps x11-xserver-utils libgl1-mesa-dri mesa-vulkan-drivers libvulkan1 \
      fonts-noto-cjk fonts-noto-color-emoji weston python3 \
 && rm -rf /var/lib/apt/lists/* \
 && useradd --create-home tester
```

```dockerfile
# The StreamFlex headless test image on Fedora 44, with SDL3 native (sdl2-compat no longer comes into
# it). It holds the Fedora equivalents of the Debian image's packages: the build dependencies, a
```

Replace the docstring's mode list (`make_fonts.py:5-12`) with:

```python
  make_fonts.py rename SRC DST OLD NEW   copy SRC to DST with its family name OLD replaced by NEW
                                         (the same length), wherever the name table spells it
  make_fonts.py ttc DST SRC...           pack each SRC (a single-face font) into one collection, face
                                         0 first; a SRC of - is a face that does not open (zeros)
  make_fonts.py blank DST FAMILY CHARS   write a TrueType font with a glyph for each character of
                                         CHARS and no other, as a symbol font has none of "Aa0";
                                         FAMILY - names no family

                                         the fallback-font checks' font of boxes
```

and its last sentence (`:16`) with `the font now starts. A blank font holds only the tables FreeType needs, its one glyph empty; a box font's glyph is one square contour."""`.

Replace `blank()` (`:75-106`) with:

```python
# A box font's glyph: one closed square contour, its points clockwise (TrueType fills a clockwise
# contour), every one on the curve
BOX_POINTS = [(50, 0), (50, 700), (750, 700), (750, 0)]


def box_glyph():
    """The glyf entry of BOX_POINTS: one contour, each point's x and y a 16-bit delta from the last"""
    xs = [x for x, _ in BOX_POINTS]
    ys = [y for _, y in BOX_POINTS]
    dx = [x - last for x, last in zip(xs, [0] + xs[:-1])]
    dy = [y - last for y, last in zip(ys, [0] + ys[:-1])]
    return struct.pack(">hhhhh", 1, min(xs), min(ys), max(xs), max(ys)) \
        + struct.pack(">HH", len(BOX_POINTS) - 1, 0) + bytes([0x01] * len(BOX_POINTS)) \
        + struct.pack(f">{len(dx)}h", *dx) + struct.pack(f">{len(dy)}h", *dy)


def font(dst, family, chars, filled):
    """A TrueType font with a glyph for each character of CHARS and no other: empty (blank), or the
    filled square of BOX_POINTS (box)"""
    glyphs = 2
    glyph = box_glyph() if filled else b""
    advance = 800 if filled else 500
    x_max = 750 if filled else 500
    head = struct.pack(">IIIIHHqqhhhhHHhhh", 0x00010000, 0x00010000, 0, 0x5F0F3CF5, 0x000B, 1000, 0, 0,
                       0, 0, x_max, 700, 0, 8, 2, 0, 0)
    hhea = struct.pack(">IhhhHhhhhhhhhhhhH", 0x00010000, 800, -200, 0, advance, 0, 0, x_max, 1, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0,
                       glyphs)
    points, contours = (len(BOX_POINTS), 1) if filled else (0, 0)
    maxp = struct.pack(">IH13H", 0x00010000, glyphs, points, contours, 0, 0, 2, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0)
    hmtx = struct.pack(">HhHh", 500, 0, advance, BOX_POINTS[0][0] if filled else 0)
    loca = struct.pack(">3H", 0, 0, len(glyph) // 2)
    glyf = glyph if filled else b"\0" * 4
```

and keep the rest of the old body (from `    # cmap format 4: ...` at :84 through `open(dst, "wb").write(font)` at :106) unchanged, except that its last two lines become:

```python
                 "cmap": cmap, "name": name})
    open(dst, "wb").write(data)
```

Write `$P/box-probe.py` (it draws the box glyph through SDL3_ttf itself, the library the checks use):

```python
"""Draw U+4E2D from a box font through SDL3_ttf and count opaque pixels across its middle row"""
import ctypes
import sys

ttf = ctypes.CDLL("libSDL3_ttf.so.0")


class Surface(ctypes.Structure):
    _fields_ = [("flags", ctypes.c_uint32), ("format", ctypes.c_uint32), ("w", ctypes.c_int),
                ("h", ctypes.c_int), ("pitch", ctypes.c_int), ("pixels", ctypes.c_void_p)]


class Color(ctypes.Structure):
    _fields_ = [("r", ctypes.c_uint8), ("g", ctypes.c_uint8), ("b", ctypes.c_uint8), ("a", ctypes.c_uint8)]


ttf.TTF_Init.restype = ctypes.c_bool
ttf.TTF_OpenFont.restype = ctypes.c_void_p
ttf.TTF_OpenFont.argtypes = [ctypes.c_char_p, ctypes.c_float]
ttf.TTF_FontHasGlyph.restype = ctypes.c_bool
ttf.TTF_FontHasGlyph.argtypes = [ctypes.c_void_p, ctypes.c_uint32]
ttf.TTF_RenderGlyph_Blended.restype = ctypes.POINTER(Surface)
ttf.TTF_RenderGlyph_Blended.argtypes = [ctypes.c_void_p, ctypes.c_uint32, Color]
if not ttf.TTF_Init():
    sys.exit("TTF_Init failed")
font = ttf.TTF_OpenFont(sys.argv[1].encode(), 48.0)
if not font:
    sys.exit("the font did not open")
print("U+4E2D:", ttf.TTF_FontHasGlyph(font, 0x4E2D), "U+0041:", ttf.TTF_FontHasGlyph(font, 0x41))
s = ttf.TTF_RenderGlyph_Blended(font, 0x4E2D, Color(255, 255, 255, 255)).contents
row = (ctypes.c_uint8 * s.pitch).from_address(s.pixels + (s.h // 2) * s.pitch)
opaque = sum(1 for x in range(s.w) if row[4 * x + 3] == 255)   # ARGB8888, little-endian: alpha is byte 3
print(f"{s.w} x {s.h}, {opaque} opaque pixels across the middle row")
```

Run:

```powershell
docker run --rm -v "C:/Users/jscha/source/repos/streamflex:/src:ro" -v "C:/Users/jscha/ClaudeScratch/streamflex-sdl3-port:/out" streamflex-test bash -c 'python3 /src/tests/headless/make_fonts.py blank /out/blank-after.ttf Dingy "%&*" && python3 /src/tests/headless/make_fonts.py box /tmp/sf-box.ttf "SF Box" 中 && python3 /out/box-probe.py /tmp/sf-box.ttf; sha256sum /out/blank-before.ttf /out/blank-after.ttf'
```

Expected: `U+4E2D: True U+0041: False`, then a surface about 38 x 67 with more than 28 opaque pixels across the middle row (the square is 700 of 1000 units, 33.6 px at 48 pt), where a blank font's glyph would give 0; and the two `sha256sum` lines carry the same hash. A different hash means the `blank` output changed and the existing font checks' fixtures with it: fix it before going on.

### Task 1.2a: The straight port

SDL's migration tools rename what they can; every subsystem is then ported by hand (spec § 1 items 3, 5, 6, 7 and 8, and item 9's interim screen size); the harness moves to SDL3 on lavapipe. Nothing a person sees changes: the existing suites, unchanged in what they assert, are the proof. The renderer is created here straight from the platform's Vulkan-first list; Task 1.5 replaces that with `create_renderer()`, and Task 1.6 the interim screen size. Return values are flipped as each site is ported (the build cannot pass otherwise); Task 1.3 audits them and proves each failure branch. Key codes keep SDL2's values here; Task 1.4 adds the translation for the keys SDL3 moved.

- [ ] **Step 2: Run SDL's migration tools over `src/`**

Create `$P/migrate.sh` (LF line endings):

```bash
#!/bin/bash
# Plan Task 1.2a: SDL's own migration tools over StreamFlex's src/, at the versions the port targets
# (SDL 3.2.10's build-scripts, SDL_ttf 3.2.2's semantic patch), per SDL's docs/README-migration.md.
# Runs in the streamflex-test image with the repository at /src, READ-WRITE: it edits src/ in place.
set -eu
apt-get update > /dev/null
apt-get install -y --no-install-recommends coccinelle > /dev/null
rm -rf /tmp/sdl3 /tmp/sdl3-ttf
git clone --quiet --depth 1 --branch release-3.2.10 https://github.com/libsdl-org/SDL.git /tmp/sdl3
git clone --quiet --depth 1 --branch release-3.2.2 https://github.com/libsdl-org/SDL_ttf.git /tmp/sdl3-ttf
S=/tmp/sdl3/build-scripts
mapfile -t files < <(find /src/src -name '*.[ch]' -not -path '/src/src/external/*' | sort)

# 1. StreamFlex's own names first, while the SDL2 names they stand for are still there: the
#    integer-rectangle draws, the 32-bit ticks, the font opener, and SDL's one error text for all three
#    libraries (rename_symbols.py's single-symbol mode: whole words only)
for pair in SDL_RenderCopy:draw_texture SDL_RenderFillRect:fill_rect SDL_RenderDrawRect:outline_rect \
            SDL_RenderDrawLine:draw_line SDL_GetTicks:ticks_ms TTF_OpenFontIndex:open_font_face \
            IMG_GetError:SDL_GetError TTF_GetError:SDL_GetError TTF_SetError:SDL_SetError; do
    python3 "$S/rename_symbols.py" "${pair%%:*}" "${pair#*:}" "${files[@]}"
done

# 2. SDL's semantic patch (renames, SDL_CreateWindow's arguments, SDL_EventState, the bool returns),
#    then SDL_ttf's (its renames). A file spatch cannot parse is left to the hand port and named here
for f in "${files[@]}"; do
    spatch --sp-file "$S/SDL_migration.cocci" --in-place --very-quiet "$f" > /dev/null 2>&1 || echo "SDL cocci could not patch $f"
    spatch --sp-file /tmp/sdl3-ttf/build-scripts/SDL_migration.cocci --in-place --very-quiet "$f" > /dev/null 2>&1 \
        || echo "SDL_ttf cocci could not patch $f"
done

# 3. Whatever the semantic patch left under an SDL2 name (SDL_oldnames.h's table)
python3 "$S/rename_symbols.py" --all-symbols "${files[@]}"

# 4. The headers, then the platform macros (StreamFlex uses _WIN32, __unix__ and __linux__, which
#    rename_macros.py leaves alone: it is run for completeness and should change nothing)
python3 "$S/rename_headers.py" "${files[@]}"
python3 "$S/rename_macros.py" "${files[@]}"
echo "MIGRATED ${#files[@]} files"
```

Replace `init_sdl()` (`:237-265`) with:

```c
// A function to give the display's refresh rate in whole hertz, as SDL2 gave it: SDL3's is a float (59.94)
static int display_refresh_rate(void)
{
    return (int) (display_mode.refresh_rate + 0.5f);
}

// A function to initialize SDL
static void init_sdl()
{    
    // Set flags, hints. SDL3 has no SDL_HINT_RENDER_SCALE_QUALITY: each texture is made linear as it is
    // created (surface_texture()); the other two hints are SDL3's under the same names
    SDL_InitFlags sdl_flags = SDL_INIT_VIDEO;
#ifdef __unix__
    SDL_SetHint(SDL_HINT_VIDEO_X11_NET_WM_BYPASS_COMPOSITOR, "0");
#endif
    SDL_SetHint(SDL_HINT_VIDEO_ALLOW_SCREENSAVER, config.inhibit_os_screensaver ? "0" : "1");

    // Initialize SDL
    if (!SDL_Init(sdl_flags))
        log_fatal("Could not initialize SDL\n%s", SDL_GetError());

    // The screen size is the primary display's desktop mode: SDL3 has no display 0. Interim, until
    // Task 1.6 takes it from the window's pixels (spec section 1, item 9). A mode that cannot be read
    // leaves display_mode zero, as SDL2's failed call did
    const SDL_DisplayMode *mode = SDL_GetDesktopDisplayMode(SDL_GetPrimaryDisplay());
    if (mode != NULL)
        display_mode = *mode;
    geo.screen_width = display_mode.w;
    geo.screen_height = display_mode.h;

    // SDL reports 0 when the display does not say (Xvfb, some VMs and remote desktops), and
    // every timing below divides by the rate: create_window() reads it again from display_mode
    if (display_mode.refresh_rate <= 0.0f) {
        log_debug("The display reports no refresh rate, using %i Hz", DEFAULT_REFRESH_RATE);
        display_mode.refresh_rate = (float) DEFAULT_REFRESH_RATE;
    }
    refresh_period = 1000 / (Uint32) display_refresh_rate();
    geo.screen_margin = (int) (SCREEN_MARGIN * (float) geo.screen_height);
    geo.title_min_size = (int) (TITLE_MIN_SIZE * (float) geo.screen_height + 0.5F);
}
```

Replace `renderer_vsync()` and its comment (`:267-287`) with:

```c
// A function to tell whether the renderer really presents with VSync, by what SDL3 says it runs with.
// Interim: Task 1.5 re-bases the VSync check per renderer
static bool renderer_vsync()
{
#ifdef STREAMFLEX_TEST_HOOKS
    // Only the headless harness builds this: its SDL simulates VSync, so a refusal is made up here.
    // "off" is a renderer that keeps VSync on when it is not wanted; any other value never gives it.
    const char *refused = getenv("STREAMFLEX_TEST_VSYNC_REFUSED");
    if (refused != NULL)
        return strcmp(refused, "off") == 0;
#endif
    int vsync = 0;
    if (renderer == NULL || !SDL_GetRenderVSync(renderer, &vsync))
        return false;
    return vsync != 0;
}
```

In `apply_frame_timing()`: `:303`'s `(SDL 2.0.18)` becomes `(SDL_SetRenderVSync)`; `:308` becomes `    int rate = display_refresh_rate();`; `:333-334` become:

Replace `create_window()` (`:356-393`) with:

```c
// A function to create the window and renderer
static void create_window()
{
    window = SDL_CreateWindow(PROJECT_NAME, 0, 0, SDL_WINDOW_FULLSCREEN);
    if (window == NULL)
        log_fatal("Could not create SDL Window\n%s", SDL_GetError());
    SDL_HideCursor();

    // The renderer from the platform's list, Vulkan first: SDL takes the first that works (spec
    // section 3). Interim: Task 1.5 moves this into create_renderer(), with SDL_RENDER_DRIVER and
    // the Renderer: lines
    apply_frame_timing();
#ifdef _WIN32
    renderer = SDL_CreateRenderer(window, "vulkan,direct3d12,direct3d11,opengl");
#else
    renderer = SDL_CreateRenderer(window, "vulkan,opengl");
#endif
    if (renderer == NULL)
        log_fatal("Could not initialize renderer\n%s", SDL_GetError());
    SDL_SetRenderDrawBlendMode(renderer, SDL_BLENDMODE_BLEND);
    SDL_SetRenderVSync(renderer, vsync_wanted ? 1 : 0);   // A refusal is check_vsync()'s to report

    // Which video driver and renderer SDL chose: X11 or Wayland, Vulkan or OpenGL
    log_debug("Video: SDL's %s driver, the %s renderer", SDL_GetCurrentVideoDriver(), SDL_GetRendererName(renderer));
    check_vsync();

    // Set background color
    set_draw_color();
}
```

(SDL3 makes a fullscreen window at its display's bounds, `SDL_video.c:2473-2483` at 3.2.10, so the size 0 x 0 is never used. The HWND is no longer copied here: `win32.c` reads it from the window when it needs it, Step 9.)

```c
void print_version(FILE *stream)
{
    int sdl_version = SDL_GetVersion();
    int img_version = IMG_Version();

}
```

Replace the event loop's `switch` (`:2483-2555`) with:

```c
            switch (event.type) {
                case SDL_EVENT_QUIT:
                    quit(EXIT_SUCCESS);
                    break;

                case SDL_EVENT_KEY_DOWN:
                    ticks.last_input = ticks.main;
                    handle_keypress(event.key.key, event.key.repeat);
#ifdef STREAMFLEX_TEST_HOOKS
                    test_key_undrawn = true;
#endif
                    break;

                case SDL_EVENT_KEY_UP:
                    if (settings_is_open())
                        settings_raw_release((int) event.key.key);
                    break;

                case SDL_EVENT_MOUSE_BUTTON_DOWN:
                    if (config.mouse_select && !settings_is_open() && event.button.button == SDL_BUTTON_LEFT) {
                        ticks.last_input = ticks.main;
                        execute_command(current_entry->cmd);
                    }
                    break;

                case SDL_EVENT_JOYSTICK_ADDED: {
                    // `which` is the instance id in SDL3; the Device setting counts device indexes
                    int index = joystick_index(event.jdevice.which);
                    if (gamepad_on && index >= 0 && SDL_IsGamepad(event.jdevice.which) &&
                        (config.gamepad_device < 0 || config.gamepad_device == index))
                        connect_gamepad(index, !state.application_running, true);
                    settings_pads_changed();   // The Device row names every pad present, while settings are open
                    break;
                }

                case SDL_EVENT_JOYSTICK_REMOVED:
                    // `which` is the instance id here: only a pad in the list is removed, whatever Device says
                    log_debug("Gamepad disconnected");
                    disconnect_gamepad((int) event.jdevice.which, true, true);
                    settings_pads_changed();
                    break;

                case SDL_EVENT_WINDOW_FOCUS_LOST:
                    log_debug("Lost keyboard focus");
                    state.has_focus = false;
                    if (state.application_launching) {
                        log_debug("Application detected");
                        state.application_launching = false;
                        state.application_running = true;
                        pre_launch();
                    }
#ifdef _WIN32
                    // Sometimes the launcher will lose focus on Windows when autostarting
                    // So if we lose the window focus within 10 seconds of the launcher starting
                    // we will grab back th Window focus. This is a bit of a hack
                    else if (ticks.main - ticks.program_start < 10000)
                        set_foreground_window();
#endif
                    break;

                case SDL_EVENT_WINDOW_FOCUS_GAINED:
                    log_debug("Gained keyboard focus");
                    state.has_focus = true;
#ifdef _WIN32
                    retry_exit_hotkey_events(true);   // The application that held its key may have just quit
#endif
                    break;

                case SDL_EVENT_WINDOW_MOUSE_LEAVE:
                    log_debug("Lost mouse focus");
                    break;
            }
```

(SDL3 key events carry the unmodified key code by default: `SDL_GetKeyFromScancode(scancode, modstate, true)` sets the modifiers to none for key events, `SDL_keyboard.c:476-493` at 3.2.10, so Shift+A still reads as `SDLK_A`, `0x61`, as on SDL2. Text input stays off: nothing calls `SDL_StartTextInput`, spec § 1 item 6.)

- [ ] **Step 5: `launcher.c`: gamepads by instance id, and the virtual pads**

SDL3 has no device index. Before `init_gamepad()` (`:1623`) add:

```c
// SDL3 names joysticks by instance id alone. A device index, as the Device setting and the log name
// one, is a joystick's place in the list SDL_GetJoysticks() gives now, as SDL2's device index was.
// A function to get the joystick at a device index now; 0, which no joystick has, when there is none
static SDL_JoystickID joystick_at(int device_index)
{
    int count = 0;
    SDL_JoystickID *ids = SDL_GetJoysticks(&count);
    SDL_JoystickID id = ids != NULL && device_index >= 0 && device_index < count ? ids[device_index] : 0;
    SDL_free(ids);
    return id;
}

// A function to find a joystick's device index now, from its instance id; -1 when it is gone
static int joystick_index(SDL_JoystickID id)
{
    int count = 0;
    SDL_JoystickID *ids = SDL_GetJoysticks(&count);
    int index = -1;
    for (int i = 0; ids != NULL && i < count && index < 0; i++) {
        if (ids[i] == id)
            index = i;
    }
    SDL_free(ids);
    return index;
}

// A function to count the joysticks present
static int joystick_count(void)
{
    int count = 0;
    SDL_JoystickID *ids = SDL_GetJoysticks(&count);
    if (ids == NULL)
        return 0;
    SDL_free(ids);
    return count;
}
```

Replace the test hooks' state and attach (`:1739-1770`) with:

```c
static SDL_Joystick *test_pad = NULL;   // The harness's virtual gamepad, while attached
static SDL_JoystickID test_pad_id = 0;  // Its instance id; 0, which no joystick has, while none is attached
static SDL_JoystickID test_swap[3] = { 0, 0, 0 };   // STREAMFLEX_TEST_PAD_SWAP's pads A, B and C
static SDL_JoystickID test_plug = 0;    // STREAMFLEX_TEST_PAD_PLUG's pad, while it is plugged in
static int test_swap_step = 0;
static bool test_pad_file = false;      // STREAMFLEX_TEST_PAD's file was there last frame
static int test_pad_frames = 0;         // Frames STREAMFLEX_TEST_PAD_FRAMES's press has left

// A function only the headless harness builds: attach a virtual gamepad with every button and axis
// SDL3 has, which SDL maps to them in their order (its masks left 0, SDL_virtualjoystick.c:201-222 at
// 3.2.10), so joystick button N is gamepad button N, as on SDL2. Its instance id; 0 when it failed.
static SDL_JoystickID test_pad_attach_virtual(void)
{
    SDL_VirtualJoystickDesc desc;
    SDL_INIT_INTERFACE(&desc);
    desc.type = (Uint16) SDL_JOYSTICK_TYPE_GAMEPAD;
    desc.naxes = (Uint16) SDL_GAMEPAD_AXIS_COUNT;
    desc.nbuttons = (Uint16) SDL_GAMEPAD_BUTTON_COUNT;
    return SDL_AttachVirtualJoystick(&desc);
}

// A function only the headless harness builds: with STREAMFLEX_TEST_PAD set, it attaches the
// virtual gamepad once the gamepad runs
static void test_pad_attach()
{
    if (getenv("STREAMFLEX_TEST_PAD") == NULL || !gamepad_on || test_pad_id != 0)
        return;
    test_pad_id = test_pad_attach_virtual();
    test_pad = test_pad_id != 0 ? SDL_OpenJoystick(test_pad_id) : NULL;
    if (test_pad == NULL)
        log_error("Test hook: no virtual gamepad\n%s", SDL_GetError());
}
```

- [ ] **Step 6: `image.c` and `image.h`: surfaces, textures, text and the draw helpers**

Includes `:6-8` become `#include <SDL3/SDL.h>`, `#include <SDL3_image/SDL_image.h>`, `#include <SDL3_ttf/SDL_ttf.h>`. After `quit_svg()` (`:54`) add:

```c

// A function to make a texture from a surface, filtered linearly when it is drawn scaled, as SDL2's
// SDL_HINT_RENDER_SCALE_QUALITY "1" asked for every texture. SDL3 has no such hint, and is linear by
// default, but each texture is set anyway, whatever the default becomes (spec section 1, item 8).
// NULL when the texture could not be made, with SDL's reason still in SDL_GetError()
SDL_Texture *surface_texture(SDL_Surface *surface)
{
    SDL_Texture *texture = SDL_CreateTextureFromSurface(renderer, surface);
    if (texture != NULL)
        SDL_SetTextureScaleMode(texture, SDL_SCALEMODE_LINEAR);
    return texture;
}

// A function to open a font file at a point size and face: SDL3_ttf takes the face as a property
// (TTF_OpenFontIndex is gone, spec section 1, item 5)
TTF_Font *open_font_face(const char *path, int size, int face)
{
    SDL_PropertiesID props = SDL_CreateProperties();
    SDL_SetStringProperty(props, TTF_PROP_FONT_CREATE_FILENAME_STRING, path);
    SDL_SetFloatProperty(props, TTF_PROP_FONT_CREATE_SIZE_FLOAT, (float) size);
    SDL_SetNumberProperty(props, TTF_PROP_FONT_CREATE_FACE_NUMBER, face);
    TTF_Font *font = TTF_OpenFontWithProperties(props);
    SDL_DestroyProperties(props);
    return font;
}

// The layout is in whole pixels and SDL3 draws with float rectangles. These convert at the draw call
// only, a cast of whole numbers, so nothing lands half a pixel off (spec section 1, item 3). A NULL
// rectangle is the whole texture, or the whole target, as in SDL.
void draw_texture(SDL_Renderer *r, SDL_Texture *texture, const SDL_Rect *src, const SDL_Rect *dst)
{
    SDL_FRect from, to;
    if (src != NULL)
        SDL_RectToFRect(src, &from);
    if (dst != NULL)
        SDL_RectToFRect(dst, &to);
    SDL_RenderTexture(r, texture, src != NULL ? &from : NULL, dst != NULL ? &to : NULL);
}

void draw_texture_rotated(SDL_Renderer *r, SDL_Texture *texture, const SDL_Rect *dst, double angle, SDL_FlipMode flip)
{
    SDL_FRect to;
    SDL_RectToFRect(dst, &to);
    SDL_RenderTextureRotated(r, texture, NULL, &to, angle, NULL, flip);
}

void fill_rect(SDL_Renderer *r, const SDL_Rect *rect)
{
    SDL_FRect area;
    if (rect != NULL)
        SDL_RectToFRect(rect, &area);
    SDL_RenderFillRect(r, rect != NULL ? &area : NULL);
}

void outline_rect(SDL_Renderer *r, const SDL_Rect *rect)
{
    SDL_FRect area;
    SDL_RectToFRect(rect, &area);
    SDL_RenderRect(r, &area);
}

void draw_line(SDL_Renderer *r, int x1, int y1, int x2, int y2)
{
    SDL_RenderLine(r, (float) x1, (float) y1, (float) x2, (float) y2);
}
```

(The renderer parameter is `r`: `image.c` declares the global `renderer`, and MSVC's `/W4` refuses a parameter that hides it, C4459.)

```c
// A function to name the gamepads present for the Device row, by their device index now (the
// launcher opens them by instance id, so an index is only good at the moment it is listed): every
// joystick SDL lists while the gamepad runs, as SDL names it. A nameless one, or a failed list, is
// the model's to describe ("Pad N", or none).
static void list_pads(void)
{
    const char *names[SETTINGS_MAX_PADS] = { NULL };
    int count = 0;
    SDL_JoystickID *ids = gamepad_running() ? SDL_GetJoysticks(&count) : NULL;
    if (ids == NULL)
        count = 0;
    if (count > SETTINGS_MAX_PADS)
        count = SETTINGS_MAX_PADS;
    for (int i = 0; i < count; i++)
        names[i] = SDL_GetJoystickNameForID(ids[i]);
    settings_set_pads(model, names, count);   // It copies the names
    SDL_free(ids);
}
```

- [ ] **Step 9: Windows: the HWND from the window's property, the exit hotkey through SDL's message hook**

In `platform/win32.c`: `:8-9` become `#include <SDL3/SDL.h>`; `:27` `extern SDL_SysWMinfo wm_info;` becomes `extern SDL_Window *window;`. After `directory_exists()` (`:52`) add:

```c

// A function to get a window's HWND, from its SDL3 property (SDL3 has no SDL_SysWMinfo). It is read
// each time: the OpenGL renderer replaces the native window when it starts (spec section 3)
static HWND hwnd_of(SDL_Window *w)
{
    return (HWND) SDL_GetPointerProperty(SDL_GetWindowProperties(w), SDL_PROP_WINDOW_WIN32_HWND_POINTER, NULL);
}
```

Each `wm_info.info.win.window` (`:120`, `:125`, `:137`, `:190`, `:357`, `:443`, `:464`, `:480`) becomes `hwnd_of(window)`. `:447`, `:459` `SDL_GetTicks()` become `ticks_ms()`. Replace `check_exit_hotkey()` (`:486-498`) with:

```c
static bool exit_hotkey_listening = false;   // While an app runs: its WM_HOTKEY then closes the app

// A function SDL calls with each Windows message before it handles it (SDL3 has no SDL_SYSWMEVENT):
// the exit hotkey's WM_HOTKEY, while an app runs, closes the window in front. SDL then handles every
// message as it would have (true).
static bool SDLCALL exit_hotkey_hook(void *userdata, MSG *msg)
{
    UNUSED(userdata);
    if (exit_hotkey_listening && msg->message == WM_HOTKEY) {
        log_debug("Exit hotkey detected");
        HWND hwnd = GetForegroundWindow();
        if (hwnd == NULL)
            log_error("Could not get top window");
        else
            PostMessage(hwnd, WM_CLOSE, 0, 0);
    }
    return true;
}

// A function to listen for the exit hotkey (on while an app runs) or stop; the first call sets the hook
void listen_exit_hotkey(bool on)
{
    static bool hooked = false;
    if (!hooked) {
        SDL_SetWindowsMessageHook(exit_hotkey_hook, NULL);
        hooked = true;
    }
    exit_hotkey_listening = on;
}
```

In `platform/platform.h`, `:39` becomes `void listen_exit_hotkey(bool on);   // While an app runs, the exit hotkey's WM_HOTKEY closes it`. `keycode_convert.h` is unchanged: SDL3's `SDLK_F1` ... `SDLK_F24` keep SDL2's values.

- After the Mesa block (after `echo "Mesa: $gallium, from $mesa_driver"`, `:239`) add:

```bash

# Mesa's Vulkan driver, lavapipe, which the launcher's Vulkan-first renderer list uses: found from its
# ICD file, which names the library. VK_DRIVER_FILES keeps the Vulkan loader to it alone, so no other
# ICD in the image is tried first. lavapipe always uses LLVM's JIT (llvmpipe's; softpipe has no Vulkan)
lvp_icd=$(ls /usr/share/vulkan/icd.d/lvp_icd.*.json 2> /dev/null | head -1)
lvp_driver=$(python3 -c 'import json, sys; print(json.load(open(sys.argv[1]))["ICD"]["library_path"])' "$lvp_icd" 2> /dev/null)
[ -f "$lvp_driver" ] || lvp_driver=$(ldconfig -p | awk -v name="$(basename "${lvp_driver:-none}")" '$1 == name { print $NF; exit }')
vulkan_loader=$(ldconfig -p | awk '$1 == "libvulkan.so.1" { print $NF; exit }')
[ -n "$lvp_icd" ] && [ -f "$lvp_driver" ] && [ -f "$vulkan_loader" ] \
    || { echo "NO LAVAPIPE FOUND (ICD: '$lvp_icd', driver: '$lvp_driver', loader: '$vulkan_loader')"; exit 2; }
echo "Vulkan: lavapipe, from $lvp_driver"
```

- `:241-250`'s comment gains, after its first sentence: `The Vulkan loader and lavapipe are preloaded for the same reason: SDL unloads the loader when the renderer goes, and the loader unloads lavapipe.` `:259` becomes:



Replace `checks/20-refresh.sh:1-21` (the comment and the probe) with:

```bash
# Item 22: Xvfb reports a refresh rate of 0, as some VMs and remote desktops do. The launcher
# must start, use 60 Hz and say so, instead of dividing by zero. Whether the 0 reaches it depends
# on the SDL underneath, which may stand a rate in for a display that says 0: then the launcher must
# use the rate it is given, and not claim a fallback it never made. What this SDL reports is asked,
# through the library the launcher uses, rounded to whole hertz as the launcher rounds it.
# Both branches rest on the X display itself saying 0 Hz, which is asserted first: a display that
# said 60 would make the 60 branch pass with no fallback exercised anywhere.
x_hz=$(xrandr --verbose 2> "$out/f22-xrandr.err" \
    | awk '/[*]current/ { cur = 1; next } cur && $1 == "v:" { print $NF; exit }')
sdl_hz=$(python3 - 2> "$out/f22-sdl.err" << 'EOF'
import ctypes
sdl = ctypes.CDLL("libSDL3.so.0")
class DisplayMode(ctypes.Structure):
    _fields_ = [("displayID", ctypes.c_uint32), ("format", ctypes.c_uint32), ("w", ctypes.c_int),
                ("h", ctypes.c_int), ("pixel_density", ctypes.c_float), ("refresh_rate", ctypes.c_float),
                ("refresh_rate_numerator", ctypes.c_int), ("refresh_rate_denominator", ctypes.c_int),
                ("internal", ctypes.c_void_p)]
sdl.SDL_Init.restype = ctypes.c_bool
sdl.SDL_Init.argtypes = [ctypes.c_uint32]
sdl.SDL_GetPrimaryDisplay.restype = ctypes.c_uint32
sdl.SDL_GetDesktopDisplayMode.restype = ctypes.POINTER(DisplayMode)
sdl.SDL_GetDesktopDisplayMode.argtypes = [ctypes.c_uint32]
if not sdl.SDL_Init(0x20):
    raise SystemExit(1)
mode = sdl.SDL_GetDesktopDisplayMode(sdl.SDL_GetPrimaryDisplay())
if not mode:
    raise SystemExit(1)
print(int(mode.contents.refresh_rate + 0.5))
sdl.SDL_Quit()
EOF
)
```

(The `case $sdl_hz in 0) ... 60) ...` test below it, `:22-35`, is unchanged.)
