- [ ] **Step 8: `settings_screen.c`: `settings_refresh_all()`, `settings_refresh_for_reload()` and `settings_reflow()`**

After `apply_all()` (`:390`) add the two walks of `REFRESH_ORDER`. **The reload gets its own walk, not a skip flag:** `settings_refresh_all()` stays the contract's plain "every group" for Discard's callers, and the reload's walk is the same `REFRESH_ORDER` loop with a two-case switch, so the order lives in one list and a new group reaches both walks with no second list to keep in step.

```c
// A function to refresh every group, in REFRESH_ORDER, as Discard would if every setting had changed
void settings_refresh_all(void)
{
    for (size_t i = 0; i < sizeof(REFRESH_ORDER) / sizeof(REFRESH_ORDER[0]); i++)
        run_refresh(REFRESH_ORDER[i]);
}

// A function to refresh what a reload of the whole launcher must make again (a new screen size, a reset
// or new renderer), in REFRESH_ORDER, keeping the screen's state (spec section 3): the gamepad keeps
// running, its pads open, and the screensaver keeps its dim and its transition, only its screen-sized
// layer made again at the new size. Whether settings are open or not.
void settings_refresh_for_reload(void)
{
    for (size_t i = 0; i < sizeof(REFRESH_ORDER) / sizeof(REFRESH_ORDER[0]); i++) {
        if (REFRESH_ORDER[i] == SET_REFRESH_GAMEPAD)
            continue;
        if (REFRESH_ORDER[i] == SET_REFRESH_SCREENSAVER)
            remake_screensaver_texture();
        else
            run_refresh(REFRESH_ORDER[i]);
    }
}
```

```c
// A function to measure the screen again for a new size (spec section 6), keeping the page, its
// cursor and every unsaved edit: the fonts open again at the new height, the text cache empties, the
// column and the preview are measured again, and the preview is made anew. An open picker or the
// folder browser, measured when it opened, closes as Back closes it. What was logged as last drawn is
// forgotten, so the next frame logs the page and the cursor's row again.
void settings_reflow(void)
{
    if (model == NULL)
        return;
    if (pickers_active())
        pickers_cancel();
    if (browser != NULL)
        close_browser();
    clear_text_cache();
    close_fonts();
    if (!open_fonts()) {
        close_settings();   // open_fonts() said why; the fonts opened when settings did
        return;
    }
    fitted_width = -1;
    shown_first = -1;
    shown_last = -1;
    shown_count = -1;
    drawn_note[0] = '\0';
    drawn_cursor[0] = '\0';
    logged_path[0] = '\0';
    logged_hint[0] = '\0';
    measure_layout();
    begin_pickers();
    make_preview();
    log_debug("Settings: re-measured for %ix%i px", geo.screen_width, geo.screen_height);
}
```

```c
// A function to make the screensaver's dimming layer's pixels: a screen-sized black surface; NULL when
// it cannot be made (SDL says why)
static SDL_Surface *screensaver_surface(void)
{
    SDL_Surface *surface = SDL_CreateSurface(geo.screen_width, geo.screen_height, SDL_PIXELFORMAT_ARGB8888);

}
```

(the error text unchanged; the rest of `start_screensaver()` as it is). After `reload_screensaver()` (`:830-834`) add:

```c
// A function to make the screensaver's layer again for a reload of the whole launcher (a new screen
// size, a reset or new renderer), keeping whether it dims, how far, and its transition (spec section 3:
// the screen's state is kept): reload_screensaver() would stop it, lifting the dim
void remake_screensaver_texture(void)
{
    if (screensaver == NULL)
        return;
    if (screensaver->texture != NULL)
        SDL_DestroyTexture(screensaver->texture);
    SDL_Surface *surface = screensaver_surface();
    if (surface == NULL)
        log_error("Screensaver: its layer could not be made again\n%s", SDL_GetError());
    screensaver->texture = load_texture(surface);   // NULL for no surface
    // The dim as update_screensaver() last set it: the full dim once its transition is done (alpha may
    // have stepped past it), else where the transition is, or none
    float dim = state.screensaver_active && !state.screensaver_transition ? screensaver->alpha_end_value : screensaver->alpha;
    if (screensaver->texture != NULL)
        SDL_SetTextureAlphaMod(screensaver->texture, (Uint8) dim);
    log_debug("Screensaver: its layer was made again at %ix%i px, %s", geo.screen_width, geo.screen_height,
              state.screensaver_active ? "still dimming" : "not dimming");
}
```

`update_screensaver()` (`:2131-2170`) sets the alpha mod from `screensaver->alpha` while the transition runs, sets `alpha_end_value` when it ends and then leaves it, and sets 0 when the screensaver goes off; the function above sets the same value on the new texture. In `launcher.h`, after `void reload_screensaver(void);` (`:344`) add `void remake_screensaver_texture(void);   // A reload's: the layer made again, the dim and its transition kept`.

- [ ] **Step 9: `launcher.c`: the marks, the reload, and the handlers.** Add `#include "fileio.h"` after `#include "alloc.h"` (`:22`). Add before `pre_launch()` (before `:2245`):

```c
static int reload_pending = RELOAD_NONE;   // The ReloadReasons marked since the last reload
static bool size_deferred = false;         // A size change came while an app was in front: post_launch() reads it again

// A function to tell whether an app is launching or running: nothing is laid out for a new size then
bool app_in_front(void)
{
    return state.application_launching || state.application_running;
}

// A function to mark a reload, with its reason. The handlers only mark: one reload runs after the
// events are drained (reload_run_pending()), whatever came in the batch.
void reload_mark(ReloadReason why)
{
    reload_pending |= (int) why;
}

// A function to name a reload's reasons for the log, in a fixed order
static void describe_reload(int why, char *out, size_t size)
{
    static const struct { int reason; const char *name; } names[] = {
        { RELOAD_SIZE, "the window's size" }, { RELOAD_TARGETS, "a render target reset" },
        { RELOAD_DEVICE, "a device reset or loss" }, { RELOAD_RECREATE, "a new window" }
    };
    out[0] = '\0';
    for (size_t i = 0; i < sizeof(names) / sizeof(names[0]); i++) {
        if (!(why & names[i].reason))
            continue;
        size_t used = strlen(out);
        snprintf(out + used, size - used, "%s%s", used > 0 ? " and " : "", names[i].name);
    }
}

// A function to run the one reload the marks ask for, after the events are drained and before
// anything is drawn: the screen geometry first, then every texture in the order Discard uses
// (REFRESH_ORDER), the gamepad left running and the screensaver's dim kept. While an app is launching or running, geo stays as it is and a size change waits
// for post_launch(), so a game's temporary resolution never becomes the layout. A reload with no
// renderer waits for the new one.
void reload_run_pending(void)
{
    if (reload_pending == RELOAD_NONE || renderer == NULL)
        return;
    int why = reload_pending;
    reload_pending = RELOAD_NONE;
    if (app_in_front() && (why & RELOAD_SIZE))
        size_deferred = true;
    bool resized = !app_in_front() && read_screen_geometry();
    if (why == RELOAD_SIZE && !resized) {
        log_debug("Screen: %ix%i px again; nothing to reload", geo.screen_width, geo.screen_height);
        return;
    }
    if (resized) {
        refresh_effective();      // The percentages of the screen, worked out again
        calculate_layout_area();
    }
    settings_refresh_for_reload();   // REFRESH_ORDER, the gamepad kept running and the screensaver's dim kept
    if (settings_is_open() && resized)
        settings_reflow();
    else if (resized)
        trim_title_fonts();
    char reasons[128];
    describe_reload(why, reasons, sizeof(reasons));
    log_debug("Reload: done at %ix%i px, for %s", geo.screen_width, geo.screen_height, reasons);
}

// A function to act on a change of the window's pixel size (SDL_EVENT_WINDOW_PIXEL_SIZE_CHANGED): the
// size laid out already does nothing (the event SDL sends as it makes the window, a Wayland scale
// change that keeps the native size); a new one marks the reload, or waits while an app is in front
static void pixel_size_changed(void)
{
    int width, height;
    window_pixel_size(&width, &height);
    SizeDecision decision = derive_size_decision(geo.screen_width, geo.screen_height, width, height, app_in_front(), false);
    if (decision == SIZE_RELOAD) {
        log_debug("Screen: %ix%i px, was %ix%i; reloading", width, height, geo.screen_width, geo.screen_height);
        reload_mark(RELOAD_SIZE);
    }
    else if (decision == SIZE_WAIT) {
        log_debug("Screen: %ix%i px, was %ix%i; reloading when the app is done", width, height, geo.screen_width, geo.screen_height);
        size_deferred = true;
    }
}

// A function to read the size again once an app is done (post_launch(), or a launch given up on),
// when a size change came while it was in front: the reload runs only if the size is still new, so a
// game that changes the resolution and puts it back costs nothing
static void recheck_deferred_size(void)
{
    if (!size_deferred)
        return;
    size_deferred = false;
    int width, height;
    window_pixel_size(&width, &height);
    if (derive_size_decision(geo.screen_width, geo.screen_height, width, height, false, false) == SIZE_RELOAD) {
        log_debug("Screen: %ix%i px, was %ix%i; reloading", width, height, geo.screen_width, geo.screen_height);
        reload_mark(RELOAD_SIZE);
    }
    else
        log_debug("Screen: %ix%i px again; nothing to reload", width, height);
}

// A function to follow the window to another display (SDL_EVENT_WINDOW_DISPLAY_CHANGED): its refresh
// rate, and the frame timing worked out from it again. A new size comes as its own event.
static void display_changed(SDL_DisplayID display)
{
    read_refresh_rate();
    apply_frame_timing();
    log_debug("Screen: now on display %u, %i Hz", (unsigned int) display, display_rate);
}

// A function to log a change of the OS's scale (SDL_EVENT_WINDOW_DISPLAY_SCALE_CHANGED), which is
// never applied; one that changed the pixel size too is the size event's to report
static void display_scale_changed(void)
{
    float scale = SDL_GetWindowDisplayScale(window);
    int width, height;
    window_pixel_size(&width, &height);
    if (scale != display_scale && width == geo.screen_width && height == geo.screen_height)
        log_debug("Screen: display scale %.2f, was %.2f; nothing to redo", (double) scale, (double) display_scale);
    display_scale = scale;
}
```

In `post_launch()`, after `if (config.on_launch == ON_LAUNCH_BLANK) set_draw_color();` (`:2270-2271`) add `recheck_deferred_size();`.

```c
// A function only the headless harness builds: with STREAMFLEX_TEST_APP set, an app takes the screen
// while the file it names exists, as a launched one does (launching, then the window loses the
// keyboard), and gives it back when the file goes. The focus events are pushed, so the real handlers
// run: Xvfb has no window manager to move the keyboard.
static void test_app_update()
{
    static bool in_front = false;
    const char *path = getenv("STREAMFLEX_TEST_APP");
    if (path == NULL || file_exists(path) == in_front)
        return;
    in_front = !in_front;
    if (in_front) {
        log_debug("Test hook: an app takes the screen");
        state.application_launching = true;
        ticks.application_launched = ticks.main;
    }
    else
        log_debug("Test hook: the app is done");
    SDL_Event focus;
    SDL_zero(focus);
    focus.type = in_front ? SDL_EVENT_WINDOW_FOCUS_LOST : SDL_EVENT_WINDOW_FOCUS_GAINED;
    focus.window.windowID = SDL_GetWindowID(window);
    if (!SDL_PushEvent(&focus))
        log_error("Test hook: the focus event was not pushed\n%s", SDL_GetError());
}

// A function only the headless harness builds: with STREAMFLEX_TEST_PIXEL_SIZE set, the file it names
// holds the window's size in pixels as "WxH", which stands in for SDL's while the file exists: SDL's
// X11 driver may not report a resize of a fullscreen window with no window manager. True when it did.
static bool test_pixel_size(int *width, int *height)
{
    const char *path = getenv("STREAMFLEX_TEST_PIXEL_SIZE");
    if (path == NULL)
        return false;
    size_t length = 0;
    char *text = fileio_read_all(path, &length);
    int w = 0, h = 0;
    bool forced = text != NULL && sscanf(text, "%ix%i", &w, &h) == 2;
    alloc_free(text);
    if (forced) {
        *width = w;
        *height = h;
    }
    return forced;
}

// A function only the headless harness builds: when what STREAMFLEX_TEST_PIXEL_SIZE's file holds
// changes, SDL_EVENT_WINDOW_PIXEL_SIZE_CHANGED is pushed with it, so the real handler runs
static void test_pixel_size_update()
{
    static int last_width = 0, last_height = 0;
    int width = 0, height = 0;
    if (!test_pixel_size(&width, &height) || (width == last_width && height == last_height))
        return;
    last_width = width;
    last_height = height;
    log_debug("Test hook: the window's size reads %ix%i px", width, height);
    SDL_Event resized;
    SDL_zero(resized);
    resized.type = SDL_EVENT_WINDOW_PIXEL_SIZE_CHANGED;
    resized.window.windowID = SDL_GetWindowID(window);
    resized.window.data1 = width;
    resized.window.data2 = height;
    if (!SDL_PushEvent(&resized))
        log_error("Test hook: the size event was not pushed\n%s", SDL_GetError());
}

// A function only the headless harness builds: with STREAMFLEX_TEST_DISPLAY_EVENT set, once the file it
// names exists, SDL_EVENT_WINDOW_DISPLAY_CHANGED is pushed once for the window's own display, as a move
// to another display would send: Xvfb has one display
static void test_display_event_update()
{
    static bool pushed = false;
    const char *path = getenv("STREAMFLEX_TEST_DISPLAY_EVENT");
    if (path == NULL || pushed || !file_exists(path))
        return;
    pushed = true;
    log_debug("Test hook: the window moved to a display");
    SDL_Event moved;
    SDL_zero(moved);
    moved.type = SDL_EVENT_WINDOW_DISPLAY_CHANGED;
    moved.window.windowID = SDL_GetWindowID(window);
    moved.window.data1 = (Sint32) SDL_GetDisplayForWindow(window);
    if (!SDL_PushEvent(&moved))
        log_error("Test hook: the display event was not pushed\n%s", SDL_GetError());
}
```

In `CONTRIBUTING.md`'s hook list, in its alphabetical places (before `STREAMFLEX_TEST_CAPTURE_SECONDS` at `:45`, after `STREAMFLEX_TEST_DECODE_DELAY_MS` at `:48`, and after `STREAMFLEX_TEST_PAD_SWAP` at `:62`), add:

```markdown
- `STREAMFLEX_TEST_APP=<file>`: an app takes the screen while `<file>` exists, as a launched one does (launching, then a lost keyboard), and gives it back when the file goes; the focus events are pushed, so the real handlers run. Check 47 sets it.
- `STREAMFLEX_TEST_DISPLAY_EVENT=<file>`: once `<file>` exists, `SDL_EVENT_WINDOW_DISPLAY_CHANGED` is pushed once for the window's display, as a move to another display sends. Check 47 sets it.
- `STREAMFLEX_TEST_PIXEL_SIZE=<file>`: while `<file>` exists, the `WxH` it holds is the window's size in pixels in place of SDL's, and each change of it pushes `SDL_EVENT_WINDOW_PIXEL_SIZE_CHANGED`: SDL's X11 driver may not report a resize of a fullscreen window with no window manager. Check 47 sets it.
```

### Task 1.7a: Render resets mark the one reload (spec § 3 Device loss, `TARGETS_RESET` and `DEVICE_RESET`)

**Files:**
- Modify: `src/launcher.c`: the hook prototypes (`:51-57`), new `render_reset()` beside Task 1.6b's handlers, the event switch, `reload_run_pending()` (Task 1.6b's), the hooks after `test_pad_stop()` (`:1894`), the loop's hook calls (`:2479-2481`)
- Modify: `src/settings_screen.c` (the includes `:4-26`, new functions after `settings_reflow()`), `src/settings_screen.h`
- Create: `tests/headless/checks/48-device-loss.sh`, `tests/headless/fixtures/f48-loss.ini`, `tests/headless/fixtures/f48-dim.ini`
- Modify: `CONTRIBUTING.md:45-66` (the test hooks)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `reload_mark()`, `reload_run_pending()`, `app_in_front()`, `reload_pending`, `make_preview()`, the `STREAMFLEX_TEST_APP` and `STREAMFLEX_TEST_PIXEL_SIZE` hooks (Task 1.6b).
- Produces (additions, not in the skeleton), in `settings_screen.c`, declared in `settings_screen.h`:
  ```c
  void settings_drop_textures(void);    // Before the renderer goes: the text cache, the preview, the font samples, the browsed image
  void settings_remake_textures(void);  // On a reset or new renderer: drop, then make them again; page, cursor, picker and edits kept
  ```
  and in `launcher.c` `static void render_reset(ReloadReason why)`, the log line `Renderer: device lost; reloading`, and the test hook `STREAMFLEX_TEST_RENDER_EVENT=<file>`, whose words `targets`, `reset` and `lost` Task 1.7b's checks use too.

**Why settings get a textures-only rebuild and not `settings_reflow()`.** A reset keeps the screen's size, so nothing needs measuring again, and spec § 3 keeps "the current menu and screen state": an open picker or folder browser stays open. `settings_reflow()` closes them, which only a new size justifies. So a reset or a new renderer at the same size runs `settings_remake_textures()`, and a new size runs `settings_reflow()`, which makes the preview anew too.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing checks, `tests/headless/checks/48-device-loss.sh`**

```bash
# Device loss (spec section 3): SDL's three render events, posted by a test hook
# (STREAMFLEX_TEST_RENDER_EVENT), since no software renderer sends them. A target or device reset runs
# the full reload a config change runs, once per batch of events, after they are drained: every
# texture made again, the menu and the screen kept, and the screen redrawn to the pixel. With
# settings open, their textures are made again and the page stays. While an app holds a size change
# back, a reset rebuilds the textures at the geometry in force; the size waits for the app.
D48_EVENT=/tmp/d48-event D48_SIZE=/tmp/d48-size D48_APP=/tmp/d48-app
rm -f "$out/d48.path"
d48_send() { printf '%s\n' "$1" > "$D48_EVENT"; }
# The background's corner and the box the highlight's #FF00FF spans, once the frame is on screen,
# kept under TAG; d48_same says both match before and after
d48_box() {
    local name=$1 tag=$2 shot=/tmp/d48.xwd
    sleep 1
    screen_shows "$name" "$tag" 10,10=30,45,60 && echo "$tag corner yes" >> "$out/$name.seen"
    xwd -root -silent -out "$shot" && python3 "$HERE/pixels.py" "$shot" count 255,0,255 inside 255,0,255 \
        | sed "s/^/$tag /" >> "$out/$name.boxes"
}
d48_boxline() { sed -n "s/^$2 //p" "$out/$1.boxes" 2> /dev/null | head -1; }
d48_same() {
    [ -n "$(d48_boxline "$1" before)" ] && [ "$(d48_boxline "$1" before)" = "$(d48_boxline "$1" after)" ] \
        && grep -qx 'before corner yes' "$out/$1.seen" && grep -qx 'after corner yes' "$out/$1.seen"
}
d48_before() { wait_line 'Begin program loop' "$2" && d48_box "$1" before; }
d48_after() { wait_line 'Reload: done at 1920x1080 px' "$2" && d48_box "$1" after; }
d48_reset() { d48_send reset; }
d48_batch() { d48_send 'targets reset'; }
d48_reset_seen() { d48_send reset; wait_line 'Reload: done at ' "$2"; }
d48_app_in() { : > "$D48_APP"; wait_line 'Application detected' "$2"; }
d48_app_out() { rm -f "$D48_APP"; wait_line 'Application finished' "$2"; }
d48_at() { grep -nxF -- "$2" "$out/$1.log" | head -1 | cut -d: -f1; }
# A resize as 47-screen.sh's s47_resize makes one: SDL's event first, the size hook when SDL sends none
d48_resize() {
    local name=$1 pid=$2 size=$3 line=$4 i
    if [ "$(cat "$out/d48.path" 2> /dev/null)" != hook ]; then
        xdotool search --name '^StreamFlex$' windowsize %@ "${size%x*}" "${size#*x}" > /dev/null 2>&1
        for i in $(seq 25); do
            grep -qxF -- "$line" "$LOG" 2> /dev/null && { echo event > "$out/d48.path"; return 0; }
            sleep 0.2
        done
        xdotool search --name '^StreamFlex$' windowsize %@ 1920 1080 > /dev/null 2>&1
        echo hook > "$out/d48.path"
    fi
    echo "$size" > "$D48_SIZE"
    wait_line "$line" "$pid"
}
d48_to_720_later() { d48_resize "$1" "$2" 1280x720 'Screen: 1280x720 px, was 1920x1080; reloading when the app is done'; }
d48_reset_runs() { rm -f "$D48_EVENT" "$D48_SIZE" "$D48_APP" "$out/$1.boxes" "$out/$1.seen"; }

# A device reset: the reload runs once, every group refreshed, and the screen redraws to the pixel
d48_reset_runs f48-reset
CFG=$FX/f48-loss.ini STREAMFLEX_TEST_RENDER_EVENT=$D48_EVENT run_keys f48-reset +d48_before +d48_reset +d48_after
log=$out/f48-reset.log
ok=1
[ "$(grep -cx 'Renderer: device lost; reloading' "$log")" = 1 ] && [ "$(grep -c '^Reload: done at ' "$log")" = 1 ] \
    && grep -qx 'Reload: done at 1920x1080 px, for a device reset or loss' "$log" \
    && in_range "$log" 'Renderer: device lost; reloading' 'Reload: done at ' 'Settings: refreshed the title font' \
    && in_range "$log" 'Renderer: device lost; reloading' 'Reload: done at ' 'Settings: refreshed the layout' \
    && ! in_range "$log" 'Renderer: device lost; reloading' 'Reload: done at ' 'Gamepad stopped' \
    && [ "$(grep -cx 'Renderer: vulkan' "$log")" = 1 ] && d48_same f48-reset && ran_clean f48-reset && ok=0
result "device: a reset reloads once, leaves the gamepad running, and redraws to the pixel (exit $(cat "$out/f48-reset.code"))" $ok
sed 's/^/      /' "$out/f48-reset.boxes" 2> /dev/null

# A reset while the screensaver dims: the reload makes its layer again and keeps it dimming (spec
# section 3: the screen's state is kept), so the frame stays dimmed, and the screensaver neither stops
# nor comes on again. The background #1E2D3C under the default 70 % dim reads about 9,14,18.
d48_dimmed() {
    wait_line 'Screensaver on' "$2" && sleep 3 && screen_shows "$1" before 10,10=9,14,18 && echo 'before dim yes' >> "$out/$1.seen"
}
d48_dim_after() {
    wait_line 'Reload: done at ' "$2" && screen_shows "$1" after 10,10=9,14,18 && echo 'after dim yes' >> "$out/$1.seen"
}
d48_reset_runs f48-dim
CFG=$FX/f48-dim.ini STREAMFLEX_TEST_RENDER_EVENT=$D48_EVENT run_keys f48-dim +d48_dimmed +d48_reset +d48_dim_after
log=$out/f48-dim.log
ok=1
grep -qx 'before dim yes' "$out/f48-dim.seen" && grep -qx 'after dim yes' "$out/f48-dim.seen" \
    && grep -qx 'Screensaver: its layer was made again at 1920x1080 px, still dimming' "$log" \
    && [ "$(grep -cx 'Screensaver on' "$log")" = 1 ] && ! grep -qx 'Screensaver off' "$log" \
    && ! in_range "$log" 'Renderer: device lost; reloading' 'Reload: done at ' 'Screensaver stopped' \
    && ran_clean f48-dim && ok=0
result "device: a reset while the screensaver dims keeps it dimming (exit $(cat "$out/f48-dim.code"))" $ok
sed 's/^/      /' "$out/f48-dim.seen" 2> /dev/null
grep -E '^Screensaver' "$log" | sed 's/^/      /'

# A target reset and a device reset in one batch of events: one reload, for both
d48_reset_runs f48-batch
CFG=$FX/f48-loss.ini STREAMFLEX_TEST_RENDER_EVENT=$D48_EVENT run_keys f48-batch +d48_before +d48_batch +d48_after
log=$out/f48-batch.log
ok=1
[ "$(grep -cx 'Renderer: device lost; reloading' "$log")" = 1 ] && [ "$(grep -c '^Reload: done at ' "$log")" = 1 ] \
    && grep -qx 'Reload: done at 1920x1080 px, for a render target reset and a device reset or loss' "$log" \
    && d48_same f48-batch && ran_clean f48-batch && ok=0
result "device: a target reset and a device reset in one batch reload once (exit $(cat "$out/f48-batch.code"))" $ok

# A new size and a device reset in one batch: one reload, geometry first, for both. Both hook files
# appear at once (a folder renamed into place), so both events are pushed before the same drain; the
# size is the hook's whichever path 47-screen.sh found, since only the hook can be timed this way.
d48_both() {
    rm -rf /tmp/d48-both /tmp/d48-both.new
    mkdir -p /tmp/d48-both.new
    echo 1280x720 > /tmp/d48-both.new/size
    echo reset > /tmp/d48-both.new/event
    mv /tmp/d48-both.new /tmp/d48-both
    wait_line 'Reload: done at ' "$2"
    sleep 1
}
rm -rf /tmp/d48-both /tmp/d48-both.new
CFG=$FX/f48-loss.ini STREAMFLEX_TEST_PIXEL_SIZE=/tmp/d48-both/size STREAMFLEX_TEST_RENDER_EVENT=/tmp/d48-both/event \
    run_keys f48-sizereset +d48_both
log=$out/f48-sizereset.log
ok=1
[ "$(grep -c '^Reload: done at ' "$log")" = 1 ] \
    && grep -qx "Reload: done at 1280x720 px, for the window's size and a device reset or loss" "$log" \
    && ran_clean f48-sizereset && ok=0
result "device: a new size and a device reset in one batch reload once, geometry first (exit $(cat "$out/f48-sizereset.code"))" $ok
grep -E '^(Screen|Reload|Renderer):' "$log" | sed 's/^/      /'

# With settings open on the Background page: the reset makes their textures again (the preview anew,
# placed where it was), the preview shows the background, and the page and its cursor stay
d48_settings_after() {
    wait_line 'Reload: done at 1920x1080 px' "$2" && look "$1" "$2" after 'Reload: done at 1920x1080 px' 30,30=30,45,60 @5,1074=11,22,32
}
d48_reset_runs f48-settings
CFG=$FX/f48-loss.ini STREAMFLEX_TEST_RENDER_EVENT=$D48_EVENT run_keys f48-settings Menu Down Return +d48_reset +d48_settings_after Down Menu
log=$out/f48-settings.log
ok=1
[ "$(grep -c '^Settings: the preview is at ' "$log")" = 2 ] \
    && [ "$(grep '^Settings: the preview is at ' "$log" | sort -u | wc -l)" = 1 ] \
    && grep -qx 'after yes' "$out/f48-settings.seen" \
    && sed -n '/^Reload: done at /,$p' "$log" | grep -q "^Settings: the cursor's row reads Color: " \
    && grep -q 'Settings: nothing changed' "$log" && ran_clean f48-settings && ok=0
result "device: a reset with settings open makes their textures again and keeps the page (exit $(cat "$out/f48-settings.code"))" $ok
sed 's/^/      /' "$out/f48-settings.seen" 2> /dev/null

# While an app holds a size change back, a reset rebuilds the textures at the size laid out; the new
# size is laid out once the app is done
d48_reset_runs f48-appreset
CFG=$FX/f48-loss.ini STREAMFLEX_TEST_APP=$D48_APP STREAMFLEX_TEST_PIXEL_SIZE=$D48_SIZE STREAMFLEX_TEST_RENDER_EVENT=$D48_EVENT \
    UNTIL='Reload: done at 1280x720 px' run_keys f48-appreset +d48_app_in +d48_to_720_later +d48_reset_seen +d48_app_out
d48_kept=$(d48_at f48-appreset 'Reload: done at 1920x1080 px, for a device reset or loss')
d48_finished=$(d48_at f48-appreset 'Application finished')
d48_resized=$(d48_at f48-appreset "Reload: done at 1280x720 px, for the window's size")
ok=1
[ -n "$d48_kept" ] && [ -n "$d48_finished" ] && [ -n "$d48_resized" ] \
    && [ "$d48_kept" -lt "$d48_finished" ] && [ "$d48_finished" -lt "$d48_resized" ] \
    && [ "$(grep -c '^Reload: done at ' "$out/f48-appreset.log")" = 2 ] && ran_clean f48-appreset && ok=0
result "device: a reset while an app holds a size back keeps the size laid out (exit $(cat "$out/f48-appreset.code"))" $ok
grep -E '^(Screen|Reload|Renderer|Application)' "$out/f48-appreset.log" | sed 's/^/      /'
```

- [ ] **Step 3: `settings_screen.c`: drop and remake the screen's textures.** Add `#include "settings_fonts.h"` after `#include "settings_pickers.h"` (`:16`). After `settings_reflow()` (Task 1.6b) add:

```c
// A function to let go of every texture the screen holds, before the renderer that made them goes (a
// lost device, Task 1.7b): the text cache, the preview, the font picker's samples and the browsed
// image. What the screen shows is kept: settings_remake_textures() makes them again.
void settings_drop_textures(void)
{
    if (model == NULL)
        return;
    clear_text_cache();
    fonts_clear_samples();
    if (preview != NULL)
        SDL_DestroyTexture(preview);
    preview = NULL;
    if (background_override != NULL)
        SDL_DestroyTexture(background_override);
    background_override = NULL;
    shown_path[0] = '\0';   // The browsed image is decoded again, if it is still wanted
}

// A function to make the screen's textures again on a reset or new renderer at the same size,
// keeping the page, its cursor, an open picker or the browser, and every unsaved edit: the preview is
// made anew and the browsed image decoded again; the cache and the font samples fill as they are drawn
void settings_remake_textures(void)
{
    if (model == NULL)
        return;
    settings_drop_textures();
    make_preview();
    start_decode();
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: `launcher.c`: the resets mark the reload, and the reload remakes settings' textures.** Beside Task 1.6b's handlers (after `display_scale_changed()`) add:

```c
// A function to mark the reload a render target or device reset needs (SDL_EVENT_RENDER_TARGETS_RESET,
// SDL_EVENT_RENDER_DEVICE_RESET): every texture made again once the events are drained, the menu and
// the screen kept. The log says so once for each reload, however many resets the batch held.
static void render_reset(ReloadReason why)
{
    if (!(reload_pending & (RELOAD_TARGETS | RELOAD_DEVICE)))
        log_debug("Renderer: device lost; reloading");
    reload_mark(why);
}
```

```c
                    break;

                case SDL_EVENT_RENDER_DEVICE_RESET:
                    render_reset(RELOAD_DEVICE);
                    break;
```

- [ ] **Step 5: The test hook.** Add `static void test_render_event_update(void);` to the hook prototypes (`:51-57`) and `test_render_event_update();` after Task 1.6b's `test_display_event_update();` in the loop. After Task 1.6b's `test_display_event_update()` add:

```c
// A function only the headless harness builds: with STREAMFLEX_TEST_RENDER_EVENT set, each time what
// the file it names holds changes, the render events it names are pushed in one batch, in its order:
// "targets" (SDL_EVENT_RENDER_TARGETS_RESET), "reset" (SDL_EVENT_RENDER_DEVICE_RESET) and "lost"
// (SDL_EVENT_RENDER_DEVICE_LOST), so the real handlers run on events no software renderer sends
static void test_render_event_update()
{
    static char last[64] = "";
    const char *path = getenv("STREAMFLEX_TEST_RENDER_EVENT");
    if (path == NULL)
        return;
    size_t length = 0;
    char *text = fileio_read_all(path, &length);
    char now[64] = "";
    if (text != NULL)
        copy_string(now, text, sizeof(now));
    alloc_free(text);
    if (strcmp(now, last) == 0)
        return;
    copy_string(last, now, sizeof(last));
    char *rest = NULL;
    for (char *word = strtok_r(now, " \n", &rest); word != NULL; word = strtok_r(NULL, " \n", &rest)) {
        SDL_Event render;
        SDL_zero(render);
        render.type = strcmp(word, "targets") == 0 ? SDL_EVENT_RENDER_TARGETS_RESET
                    : strcmp(word, "reset") == 0 ? SDL_EVENT_RENDER_DEVICE_RESET
                    : strcmp(word, "lost") == 0 ? SDL_EVENT_RENDER_DEVICE_LOST : 0;
        if (render.type == 0) {
            log_error("Test hook: '%s' names no render event", word);
            continue;
        }
        render.render.windowID = SDL_GetWindowID(window);
        log_debug("Test hook: pushed the render event '%s'", word);
        if (!SDL_PushEvent(&render))
            log_error("Test hook: the render event was not pushed\n%s", SDL_GetError());
    }
}
```

Check 48 sets it.

### Task 1.7b: A lost device: a new renderer, with retries, after a running app; the HWND read again (spec § 3 Device loss, `DEVICE_LOST`)

**Files:**
- Modify: `src/launcher.c`: the globals (after Task 1.5b's `plan_in_force`), `create_renderer()` (Task 1.5b's), `create_window()` (Task 1.2's HWND read at master `:389-392`), new functions after Task 1.7a's `render_reset()`, `post_launch()` (`:2257-2278`), the main loop (the key and mouse cases `:2488-2506`, the event switch, and the tail Task 1.6b wrote)
- Modify: `src/platform/win32.c` (`:27`, `:118-139`, `:190`, `:355-359`, `:439-484`), `src/platform/platform.h` (the Windows block)
- Modify: `tests/headless/checks/48-device-loss.sh` (append); create `tests/headless/fixtures/f48-fallback.ini`
- Modify: `CONTRIBUTING.md:45-66` (the test hooks)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `create_renderer()`, `plan_in_force` (Task 1.5b); `reload_mark()`, `app_in_front()` (Task 1.6b); `settings_drop_textures()`, `render_reset()`, `STREAMFLEX_TEST_RENDER_EVENT` (Task 1.7a).
- Produces, in `src/platform/win32.c` (declared in `platform.h`, Windows only), as the skeleton gives it:
  ```c
  void platform_window_changed(SDL_Window *old_window, SDL_Window *new_window);   // UnregisterHotKey on the old HWND, every hotkey on the new one, and the taskbar mark when the new window is Transparent
  ```
  This task writes the hotkey half; **the taskbar mark is Task 1.8's to add**, since `taskbar_mark_fullscreen()` is Task 1.8's and no window is Transparent before it.
- Produces, in `launcher.c`, as the skeleton gives them: the device-loss state, `renderer_lost()` and the retry schedule:
  ```c
  #define RENDERER_RETRIES 5
  #define RENDERER_RETRY_MS 2000
  static void renderer_lost(void);    // SDL_EVENT_RENDER_DEVICE_LOST: every texture, then the renderer; a new one owed
  static void renderer_retry(void);   // Each pass of the main loop: the owed renderer's next attempt, when it is due
  ```
- Also produces: the log lines `Renderer: device lost and not recovered; creating a new renderer`, `Renderer: creating a new renderer, attempt <n> of 5`, `Renderer: dropped <n> textures before the renderer went` and `Key ignored: there is no renderer yet`; the test hooks `STREAMFLEX_TEST_RENDERER_SKIP=<name>` and `STREAMFLEX_TEST_RENDERER_FAIL=<n>`; in `win32.c` the static `window_hwnd`, every Windows call's one HWND.

**What happens, in order.** `SDL_EVENT_RENDER_DEVICE_LOST` drops every texture StreamFlex holds (destroyed while their renderer still exists, the holders kept), destroys the renderer, and owes a new one. A second loss in the batch finds no renderer and does nothing. While no renderer exists the main loop pumps events, runs the launch's timeout, and draws and updates nothing; a key or click is ignored (nothing is on screen to act on). The owed renderer is attempted at once, or, while an app is launching or running, once `post_launch()` (or the launch's timeout) says the app is done: `post_launch()` makes it due, and the attempt runs in the same pass of the loop, after `Application finished`. Each attempt logs its number and makes the renderer as at startup, from `plan_in_force`; a failed one is tried again 2 s later, the loop pumping events in between; the fifth failure takes the startup error path. A new renderer reads the HWND again on Windows (`platform_window_changed(window, window)`: the OpenGL renderer replaces the native window) and marks `RELOAD_DEVICE`, so the one reload, geometry first, rebuilds everything.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing checks.** Append to `tests/headless/checks/48-device-loss.sh`:

```bash

# A lost device (SDL_EVENT_RENDER_DEVICE_LOST): every texture dropped (the fixture holds 7: three icons,
# three titles, the highlight), the renderer destroyed, a new one made from the platform's list, then
# the one reload, and the screen redrawn to the pixel. A second loss in the same batch does nothing.
d48_lost() { d48_send 'lost lost'; }
d48_reset_runs f48-lost
CFG=$FX/f48-loss.ini STREAMFLEX_TEST_RENDER_EVENT=$D48_EVENT run_keys f48-lost +d48_before +d48_lost +d48_after
log=$out/f48-lost.log
ok=1
[ "$(grep -cx 'Renderer: device lost and not recovered; creating a new renderer' "$log")" = 1 ] \
    && grep -qx 'Renderer: dropped 7 textures before the renderer went' "$log" \
    && grep -qx 'Renderer: creating a new renderer, attempt 1 of 5' "$log" \
    && ! grep -q 'attempt 2 of 5' "$log" \
    && [ "$(grep -cx 'Renderer: vulkan' "$log")" = 2 ] \
    && [ "$(grep -cx "Video: SDL's x11 driver, the vulkan renderer" "$log")" = 2 ] \
    && grep -qx 'Reload: done at 1920x1080 px, for a device reset or loss' "$log" \
    && d48_same f48-lost && ran_clean f48-lost && ok=0
result "device: a lost device gets a new Vulkan renderer and redraws to the pixel (exit $(cat "$out/f48-lost.code"))" $ok
grep -E '^(Renderer|Reload|Video):' "$log" | sed 's/^/      /'

# With settings open, a lost device's new renderer makes their textures again, and the page stays
d48_lost_settings_after() {
    wait_line 'Reload: done at 1920x1080 px' "$2" && look "$1" "$2" after 'Reload: done at 1920x1080 px' 30,30=30,45,60 @5,1074=11,22,32
}
d48_reset_runs f48-settings-lost
CFG=$FX/f48-loss.ini STREAMFLEX_TEST_RENDER_EVENT=$D48_EVENT run_keys f48-settings-lost Menu Down Return +d48_lost +d48_lost_settings_after Down Menu
log=$out/f48-settings-lost.log
ok=1
[ "$(grep -c '^Settings: the preview is at ' "$log")" = 2 ] && grep -qx 'after yes' "$out/f48-settings-lost.seen" \
    && sed -n '/^Reload: done at /,$p' "$log" | grep -q "^Settings: the cursor's row reads Color: " \
    && grep -q 'Settings: nothing changed' "$log" && ran_clean f48-settings-lost && ok=0
result "device: a lost device with settings open makes their textures again and keeps the page (exit $(cat "$out/f48-settings-lost.code"))" $ok

# A GPU that no longer offers Vulkan (the test hook leaves it out of the new renderer's list): the new
# renderer falls down the list to OpenGL, the screen keeps its size (OpenGL replaces the native window:
# no size change, no second Screen: line), and the redraw is pixel-correct
d48_reset_runs f48-skip
CFG=$FX/f48-loss.ini STREAMFLEX_TEST_RENDER_EVENT=$D48_EVENT STREAMFLEX_TEST_RENDERER_SKIP=vulkan \
    run_keys f48-skip +d48_before +d48_lost +d48_after
log=$out/f48-skip.log
ok=1
[ "$(grep -E '^Renderer: [a-z0-9]+( \([^)]*\))?$' "$log" | tr '\n' '|')" = 'Renderer: vulkan|Renderer: opengl (vulkan was unavailable)|' ] \
    && [ "$(grep -c '^Screen: ' "$log")" = 1 ] && d48_same f48-skip && ran_clean f48-skip && ok=0
result "device: a new renderer falls back to OpenGL when Vulkan is gone, at the same size (exit $(cat "$out/f48-skip.code"))" $ok
grep -E '^(Renderer|Screen):' "$log" | sed 's/^/      /'

# A device lost while an app runs: nothing more until the app is done, then the new renderer
d48_lost_seen() { d48_send lost; wait_line 'Renderer: device lost and not recovered' "$2"; sleep 3; }
d48_reset_runs f48-applost
CFG=$FX/f48-loss.ini STREAMFLEX_TEST_APP=$D48_APP STREAMFLEX_TEST_RENDER_EVENT=$D48_EVENT \
    UNTIL='Reload: done at 1920x1080 px' run_keys f48-applost +d48_app_in +d48_lost_seen +d48_app_out
log=$out/f48-applost.log
d48_finished=$(d48_at f48-applost 'Application finished')
d48_attempt=$(d48_at f48-applost 'Renderer: creating a new renderer, attempt 1 of 5')
ok=1
[ -n "$d48_finished" ] && [ -n "$d48_attempt" ] && [ "$d48_finished" -lt "$d48_attempt" ] \
    && ! in_range "$log" 'Renderer: device lost and not recovered' 'Application finished' 'creating a new renderer, attempt' \
    && [ "$(grep -cx 'Renderer: vulkan' "$log")" = 2 ] && ran_clean f48-applost && ok=0
result "device: a device lost while an app runs gets its new renderer once the app is done (exit $(cat "$out/f48-applost.code"))" $ok
grep -E '^(Renderer|Application|Reload):' "$log" | sed 's/^/      /'

# The first two attempts fail (the test hook): the third, 4 s later, makes the renderer. A key pressed
# while there is none is ignored, though its entry's command is :quit, and its frame is the first drawn
# once there is a renderer again.
d48_lost_waiting() { d48_send lost; wait_line 'Renderer: creating a new renderer, attempt 1 of 5' "$2"; }
d48_reset_runs f48-retry
CFG=$FX/f48-loss.ini STREAMFLEX_TEST_RENDER_EVENT=$D48_EVENT STREAMFLEX_TEST_RENDERER_FAIL=2 \
    run_keys f48-retry +d48_before +d48_lost_waiting '!Return' +d48_after
log=$out/f48-retry.log
ok=1
grep -qx 'Renderer: creating a new renderer, attempt 3 of 5' "$log" && ! grep -q 'attempt 4 of 5' "$log" \
    && [ "$(grep -cx 'Could not initialize renderer' "$log")" = 2 ] \
    && grep -qx 'Key ignored: there is no renderer yet' "$log" \
    && precedes "$log" 'Renderer: creating a new renderer, attempt 3 of 5' 'Quitting program' \
    && d48_same f48-retry && ran_clean f48-retry && ok=0
result "device: two failed attempts, then the third makes the renderer, and a key meanwhile is ignored (exit $(cat "$out/f48-retry.code"))" $ok
grep -E '^(Renderer|Key ignored|Could not initialize)' "$log" | sed 's/^/      /'

# Every attempt fails: after the fifth, the startup error path: SDL's reason, then exit 1
d48_reset_runs f48-giveup
CFG=$FX/f48-loss.ini STREAMFLEX_TEST_RENDER_EVENT=$D48_EVENT STREAMFLEX_TEST_RENDERER_FAIL=5 STREAMFLEX_TEST_NO_MESSAGE_BOX=1 \
    UNTIL='Quitting program' run_keys f48-giveup +d48_lost
log=$out/f48-giveup.log
ok=1
grep -qx 'Renderer: creating a new renderer, attempt 5 of 5' "$log" && ! grep -q 'attempt 6 of 5' "$log" \
    && [ "$(grep -cx 'Could not initialize renderer' "$log")" = 5 ] && ran_clean f48-giveup 1 && ok=0
result "device: five failed attempts take the startup error path, exit 1 (exit $(cat "$out/f48-giveup.code"))" $ok

# Between the attempts the loop keeps pumping events: a quit (TERM, which SDL turns into a quit event)
# right after the second attempt ends StreamFlex at once, before the third is due 2 s later
d48_reset_runs f48-quitlive
CFG=$FX/f48-loss.ini STREAMFLEX_TEST_RENDER_EVENT=$D48_EVENT STREAMFLEX_TEST_RENDERER_FAIL=5 \
    UNTIL='Renderer: creating a new renderer, attempt 2 of 5' run_keys f48-quitlive +d48_lost
log=$out/f48-quitlive.log
ok=1
grep -qx 'Renderer: creating a new renderer, attempt 2 of 5' "$log" && ! grep -q 'attempt 3 of 5' "$log" \
    && precedes "$log" 'attempt 2 of 5' 'Quitting program' && ran_clean f48-quitlive && ok=0
result "device: the quit path stays live between the attempts (exit $(cat "$out/f48-quitlive.code"))" $ok

# A fallback title is lit again after a lost device's reload (spec section 9). It needs Task 1.16's
# fallback-font fixture, the fontconfig file run.sh makes whose only font is sf-box.ttf (filled
# squares for U+4E2D and others); until that task lands this check does not run, and says so on the
# console with no result line. Task 1.16 deletes the guard. The title is U+4E2D, which only the box
# font has; its glyph is a filled box in the title's magenta, so the box it spans is nearly all
# magenta, before the loss and after.
D48_FONTS=$TESTER_HOME/fcbox/fonts.conf
if [ -f "$D48_FONTS" ]; then
    d48_reset_runs f48-fallback
    FONTCONFIG_FILE=$D48_FONTS CFG=$FX/f48-fallback.ini STREAMFLEX_TEST_RENDER_EVENT=$D48_EVENT \
        run_keys f48-fallback +d48_before +d48_lost +d48_after
    read -r d48_x0 d48_y0 d48_x1 d48_y1 d48_n <<< "$(d48_boxline f48-fallback after | sed 's/box \([0-9]*\),\([0-9]*\) to \([0-9]*\),\([0-9]*\): \([0-9]*\) .*/\1 \2 \3 \4 \5/')"
    ok=1
    d48_same f48-fallback && [ -n "${d48_n:-}" ] \
        && [ "$((2 * d48_n))" -ge "$(((d48_x1 - d48_x0 + 1) * (d48_y1 - d48_y0 + 1)))" ] \
        && ran_clean f48-fallback && ok=0
    result "device: a fallback title is lit again after a lost device's reload (exit $(cat "$out/f48-fallback.code"))" $ok
    sed 's/^/      /' "$out/f48-fallback.boxes" 2> /dev/null
else
    echo "      device: the fallback title's redraw waits for Task 1.16's $D48_FONTS"
fi
```

Each of master's eight reads of the window's handle, `wm_info.info.win.window` at `:120`, `:125`, `:137`, `:190`, `:357`, `:443`, `:464` and `:480` (in whatever form Task 1.2 ported them to), reads `window_hwnd` instead: `set_foreground_window()`, `make_window_transparent()`, `make_window_opaque()`, `start_process()`, `take_foreground()`, `register_exit_hotkey()`, `retry_exit_hotkey()` and `clear_exit_hotkey()`. Replace `register_exit_hotkey()` (`:439-451`) with:

```c
// A function to register the exit hotkey with Windows on a window. A refusal is kept, with its reason,
// so the debug log's list still names the hotkey the bindings count.
static void register_exit_hotkey_on(HWND hwnd)
{
    BOOL ret = RegisterHotKey(hwnd, 1, 0, exit_hotkey);
    if (!ret) {
        windows_error_text(GetLastError(), refused_exit_why, sizeof(refused_exit_why));
        refused_exit_code = exit_hotkey_code;
        refused_exit_ticks = SDL_GetTicks();
        exit_hotkey = 0;
        log_error("Failed to register exit hotkey with Windows: %s", refused_exit_why);
    }
}

// A function to register the exit hotkey with Windows, on the window
void register_exit_hotkey()
{
    register_exit_hotkey_on(window_hwnd);
}

// A function to read a window's native handle
static HWND hwnd_of(SDL_Window *window)
{
    return (HWND) SDL_GetPointerProperty(SDL_GetWindowProperties(window), SDL_PROP_WINDOW_WIN32_HWND_POINTER, NULL);
}

// A function to move what Windows holds for the window to the one in force: at startup (old_window
// NULL), after a new renderer (the OpenGL renderer replaces the native window, so the same SDL window
// can hold a new handle) and after a recreation. The exit hotkey, the one hotkey StreamFlex registers,
// goes from the old handle to the new one; a refusal on the new one is kept as at startup. The old
// handle is the one kept here: the old window may hold the new one now. Nothing happens when the
// handle is the same.
void platform_window_changed(SDL_Window *old_window, SDL_Window *new_window)
{
    UNUSED(old_window);
    HWND now = hwnd_of(new_window);
    if (now == window_hwnd)
        return;
    HWND was = window_hwnd;
    window_hwnd = now;
    if (was == NULL)
        return;   // Startup: register_exit_hotkey() registers on it
    if (exit_hotkey) {
        UnregisterHotKey(was, 1);
        register_exit_hotkey_on(now);
    }
    log_debug("Video: the window has a new handle; the exit hotkey moved to it");
}
```

In `src/platform/platform.h`'s Windows block, after `void register_exit_hotkey(void);` (`:36`) add:


Add `static void test_list_without(const char *list, const char *name, char *out, size_t size);` to the hook prototypes (`:51-57`), and after Task 1.7a's `test_render_event_update()`:

```c
// A function only the headless harness builds: copy a renderer list without one of its names
static void test_list_without(const char *list, const char *name, char *out, size_t size)
{
    size_t used = 0;
    out[0] = '\0';
    for (const char *at = list; *at != '\0'; ) {
        size_t length = strcspn(at, ",");
        bool skipped = length == strlen(name) && strncmp(at, name, length) == 0;
        if (!skipped && used + length + 2 <= size) {
            if (used > 0)
                out[used++] = ',';
            memcpy(out + used, at, length);
            used += length;
            out[used] = '\0';
        }
        at += length;
        if (*at == ',')
            at++;
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: `drop_textures()`, `renderer_lost()` and `renderer_retry()`.** After Task 1.7a's `render_reset()` add:

```c
// A function to destroy a texture and forget it
static void destroy_texture(SDL_Texture **texture, int *count)
{
    if (*texture == NULL)
        return;
    SDL_DestroyTexture(*texture);
    *texture = NULL;
    (*count)++;
}

// A function to destroy every texture StreamFlex holds and forget it, while the renderer that made
// them still exists: what holds each (the highlight, the clock, a menu's entries) stays, so the
// reload's stops and starts find nothing to destroy and make every texture again. Returns how many.
static int drop_textures(void)
{
    int count = 0;
    destroy_texture(&background_texture, &count);
    destroy_texture(&background_overlay, &count);
    if (slideshow != NULL)
        destroy_texture(&slideshow->transition_texture, &count);
    if (highlight != NULL) {
        destroy_texture(&highlight->texture, &count);
        highlight->button = 0;   // So the next layout renders it again
    }
    if (scroll != NULL)
        destroy_texture(&scroll->texture, &count);
    if (clk != NULL) {
        destroy_texture(&clk->time_texture, &count);
        destroy_texture(&clk->date_texture, &count);
    }
    if (screensaver != NULL)
        destroy_texture(&screensaver->texture, &count);
    for (Menu *menu = config.first_menu; menu != NULL; menu = menu->next) {
        for (unsigned int i = 0; i < menu->num_entries; i++) {
            destroy_texture(&menu->items[i]->icon, &count);
            destroy_texture(&menu->items[i]->icon_selected, &count);
            destroy_texture(&menu->items[i]->title_texture, &count);
        }
        menu->rendered_size = 0;
    }
    settings_drop_textures();   // The settings screen's own, and the browsed image
    return count;
}

// A function to let go of a renderer SDL could not recover (SDL_EVENT_RENDER_DEVICE_LOST: SDL has
// already tried once, and the renderer is unusable): every texture first, then the renderer. A new
// one is owed, at once, or once a launched app is done: a GPU reset is often the launched game's own,
// a renderer made under it would likely fail the same way, and nothing is drawn while it runs.
// A second loss in the same batch finds no renderer, and does nothing.
static void renderer_lost(void)
{
    if (renderer == NULL)
        return;
    int dropped = drop_textures();
    SDL_DestroyRenderer(renderer);
    renderer = NULL;
    log_debug("Renderer: dropped %i textures before the renderer went", dropped);
    log_debug("Renderer: device lost and not recovered; creating a new renderer");
    renderer_owed = true;
    renderer_attempt = 0;
    renderer_next_try = SDL_GetTicks();
}

// A function to make the renderer a lost device owes, when it is due: at once after the loss, or
// once a launched app is done, then RENDERER_RETRY_MS apart, up to RENDERER_RETRIES attempts (spec
// section 3). It is called once a pass of the main loop, so events are pumped between the attempts:
// the window never stops answering, and every quit path stays live. The new renderer is made as at
// startup, for the window in force; on Windows its handle is read again. The last attempt's failure
// takes the startup error path.
static void renderer_retry(void)
{
    if (!renderer_owed || app_in_front() || SDL_GetTicks() < renderer_next_try)
        return;
    renderer_attempt++;
    log_debug("Renderer: creating a new renderer, attempt %i of %i", renderer_attempt, RENDERER_RETRIES);
    if (create_renderer(&plan_in_force)) {
        renderer_owed = false;
        renderer_attempt = 0;
#ifdef _WIN32
        platform_window_changed(window, window);
#endif
        reload_mark(RELOAD_DEVICE);
        return;
    }
    if (renderer_attempt >= RENDERER_RETRIES)
        quit(EXIT_FAILURE);   // create_renderer() logged SDL's reason, as at startup
    renderer_next_try = SDL_GetTicks() + RENDERER_RETRY_MS;
}
```

(`renderer_attempt` is set back to 0 only after a renderer is made, so `create_renderer()`'s test hooks see a recreation's attempt number while it is made.)

### Task 1.8a: The window plan's helpers and the taskbar mark

Windows' Transparent window is borderless, `SDL_WINDOW_TRANSPARENT`, at the top-left corner of its display and one pixel row short of it, and the taskbar is told it covers the screen (spec § 3, "Transparent on Windows"). This task adds the pure pieces that decide its place and when a window must be replaced, and the taskbar mark in `win32.c`. Task 1.8b uses them.

**Files:**
- Modify: `src/winplan.h`, `src/winplan.c` (Task 1.5's pure module): `WindowRect`, `window_short_rect()`, `window_must_change()`
- Modify: `tests/test_winplan.c` (Task 1.5's test 14 file, CTest name `winplan`)
- Modify: `src/platform/win32.c`: `#define COBJMACROS` and `#include <shobjidl.h>` beside the includes (master `win32.c:1-18`); `taskbar_mark_fullscreen()` and `taskbar_release()` after `hide_cursor()` (master `win32.c:141-147`); one call at the end of Task 1.7's `platform_window_changed()`
- Modify: `src/platform/platform.h`: two declarations in the Windows block (master `platform.h:30-46`)
- No CMake change: the executable already links `ole32` and `uuid` on Windows (`src/CMakeLists.txt:87-88`), which hold `CoCreateInstance`, `CLSID_TaskbarList` and `IID_ITaskbarList2`.

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `WindowKind`, `WINDOW_FULLSCREEN`, `WINDOW_TRANSPARENT_SHORT` (Task 1.5, `winplan.h`); `void platform_window_changed(SDL_Window *old_window, SDL_Window *new_window)` (Task 1.7, `win32.c`).
- Produces, in `src/winplan.h` (pure, no SDL):
  ```c
  typedef struct { int x, y, w, h; } WindowRect;
  WindowRect window_short_rect(WindowRect display);
  bool window_must_change(WindowKind now, WindowKind wanted, bool transparent_failed);
  ```
- Produces, in `src/platform/win32.c` (declared in `platform.h`, Windows only):
  ```c
  void taskbar_mark_fullscreen(SDL_Window *window, bool mark);   // the skeleton's name and signature
  void taskbar_release(void);                                    // new: at quit, the taskbar's list and COM
  ```

```c
// A function to test the Transparent window's place: the display's own top-left corner, its full
// width, and one pixel row short of its height, on the first display and on one left of it and lower
static void test_window_short_rect(void)
{
    WindowRect rect = window_short_rect((WindowRect) { 0, 0, 3840, 2160 });
    CHECK_INT(rect.x, 0);
    CHECK_INT(rect.y, 0);
    CHECK_INT(rect.w, 3840);
    CHECK_INT(rect.h, 2159);
    rect = window_short_rect((WindowRect) { -1920, 120, 1920, 1080 });
    CHECK_INT(rect.x, -1920);
    CHECK_INT(rect.y, 120);
    CHECK_INT(rect.w, 1920);
    CHECK_INT(rect.h, 1079);
}

// A function to test when a window gives way to one of another kind: only when the kinds differ, and
// never to a Transparent window again once no renderer could draw one, while leaving Transparent
// always goes ahead. On Linux every background plans a fullscreen window, so nothing ever changes.
static void test_window_must_change(void)
{
    CHECK(!window_must_change(WINDOW_FULLSCREEN, WINDOW_FULLSCREEN, false));
    CHECK(!window_must_change(WINDOW_TRANSPARENT_SHORT, WINDOW_TRANSPARENT_SHORT, false));
    CHECK(window_must_change(WINDOW_FULLSCREEN, WINDOW_TRANSPARENT_SHORT, false));
    CHECK(window_must_change(WINDOW_TRANSPARENT_SHORT, WINDOW_FULLSCREEN, false));
    CHECK(!window_must_change(WINDOW_FULLSCREEN, WINDOW_TRANSPARENT_SHORT, true));
    CHECK(window_must_change(WINDOW_TRANSPARENT_SHORT, WINDOW_FULLSCREEN, true));
    for (int background = 0; background <= 3; background++)   // Color, Image, Slideshow, Transparent (launcher.h:58-63)
        CHECK(!window_must_change(WINDOW_FULLSCREEN, window_plan(background, false, NULL).kind, false));
    CHECK(window_must_change(WINDOW_FULLSCREEN, window_plan(3, true, NULL).kind, false));
}
```

```c
    test_window_must_change();
```

In `src/winplan.h`, after the `window_plan()` declaration:

```c
// A rectangle in a display's coordinates (pixels on Windows, where StreamFlex is per-monitor v2 aware)
typedef struct { int x, y, w, h; } WindowRect;

// The Transparent window's place: the display's top-left corner and one pixel row short of its bounds.
// A window that exactly covers the display is promoted to independent flip, which skips the
// compositor and with it the window's alpha (spec section 3, the spike).
WindowRect window_short_rect(WindowRect display);

// Whether the window in force (`now`) must give way to one of the kind a background wants: only when
// the kinds differ, and never to a Transparent window again once no renderer could draw one
// (`transparent_failed`, cleared when another Mode is chosen)
bool window_must_change(WindowKind now, WindowKind wanted, bool transparent_failed);
```

In `src/winplan.c`, after `window_plan()`:

```c
// A function to place the Transparent window on its display: the top-left corner, one row short
WindowRect window_short_rect(WindowRect display)
{
    return (WindowRect) { display.x, display.y, display.w, display.h - 1 };
}

// A function to tell whether the window in force must give way to one of another kind
bool window_must_change(WindowKind now, WindowKind wanted, bool transparent_failed)
{
    if (wanted == WINDOW_TRANSPARENT_SHORT && transparent_failed)
        wanted = WINDOW_FULLSCREEN;
    return wanted != now;
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Add the taskbar mark to `src/platform/win32.c`**


After the statics block (master `win32.c:28-39`):

```c

```

After `hide_cursor()` (master `win32.c:141-147`):

```c
// A function to tell the taskbar whether a window covers the screen. A Transparent window is one row
// short of its display, which keeps it out of independent flip, so Windows does not count it as
// fullscreen and would draw the taskbar over icons and titles; marked, the taskbar stays behind it.
// The taskbar's list is made at the first mark, on the main thread with COM started there, and kept
// for the window's life; the HWND comes from the window itself, since OpenGL may have replaced it.
// An opaque window is never marked, so a session that never goes Transparent never starts COM. A
// failure is logged, and the window stays unmarked.
void taskbar_mark_fullscreen(SDL_Window *window, bool mark)
{
    if (taskbar == NULL && !mark)
        return;
    HRESULT hr = S_OK;
    if (taskbar == NULL) {
        // S_OK or S_FALSE: started here, to be ended at quit. RPC_E_CHANGED_MODE: SDL started COM in
        // another mode, which serves as well and is SDL's to end.
        com_started = SUCCEEDED(CoInitializeEx(NULL, COINIT_APARTMENTTHREADED));
        ITaskbarList2 *made = NULL;
        hr = CoCreateInstance(&CLSID_TaskbarList, NULL, CLSCTX_INPROC_SERVER, &IID_ITaskbarList2, (void **) &made);
        if (SUCCEEDED(hr)) {
            hr = ITaskbarList2_HrInit(made);
            if (FAILED(hr))
                ITaskbarList2_Release(made);
        }
        if (FAILED(hr)) {
            log_error("Video: the taskbar cannot be told the window covers the screen (error 0x%08lX)", (unsigned long) hr);
            if (com_started)
                CoUninitialize();
            com_started = false;
            return;
        }
        taskbar = made;
    }
    HWND hwnd = (HWND) SDL_GetPointerProperty(SDL_GetWindowProperties(window), SDL_PROP_WINDOW_WIN32_HWND_POINTER, NULL);
    hr = hwnd != NULL ? ITaskbarList2_MarkFullscreenWindow(taskbar, hwnd, mark ? TRUE : FALSE) : E_HANDLE;
    if (FAILED(hr))
        log_error("Video: the taskbar cannot be told the window covers the screen (error 0x%08lX)", (unsigned long) hr);
    else if (mark)
        log_debug("Video: the taskbar knows the window covers the screen");
    else
        log_debug("Video: the taskbar knows the window no longer covers the screen");
}

// A function to let go of the taskbar's list at quit, and of COM when it was started for it
void taskbar_release(void)
{
    if (taskbar != NULL)
        ITaskbarList2_Release(taskbar);
    taskbar = NULL;
    if (com_started)
        CoUninitialize();
    com_started = false;
}
```

At the end of Task 1.7's `platform_window_changed()`, after it has registered the hotkeys on the new HWND, add the mark the skeleton gives that function (the call it could not make in Task 1.7, which lands before `taskbar_mark_fullscreen()` exists). A recreated window keeps its own `SDL_WINDOW_TRANSPARENT` flag (spec § 3, "OpenGL replaces the native window"), so the flag says which windows to mark:

```c
    // Every new HWND of a Transparent window is marked again; an opaque one never is
    taskbar_mark_fullscreen(new_window, (SDL_GetWindowFlags(new_window) & SDL_WINDOW_TRANSPARENT) != 0);
```

In `src/platform/platform.h`, in the `#ifdef _WIN32` block, after `void hide_cursor(Entry* entry);` (master `platform.h:45`):

```c
void taskbar_mark_fullscreen(SDL_Window *window, bool mark);   // ITaskbarList2::MarkFullscreenWindow on the window's HWND
void taskbar_release(void);                                    // At quit: the taskbar's list, and COM if it was started for it
```

### Task 1.8b: The window and its recreation

Startup makes the window the background needs, Transparent or fullscreen, with no recreation. A change of window kind while StreamFlex runs recreates the window: the new window and its renderer come first, every holder of the window moves to them, the hotkeys move, the new window takes the foreground, and only then does the old go. A Transparent window that gets no renderer falls back to an opaque one. The background clears to (0,0,0,0) on Windows in Transparent mode, and the chroma key's layered-window calls go.

**The foreground is taken before the old window is destroyed,** where the skeleton's summary of `recreate_window()` lists "destroy the old, take the foreground". The spec's reason for making the new window first (§ 3: "A process whose only window is gone loses its right to the foreground") holds only while the old window still has the foreground: Windows lets `SetForegroundWindow()` succeed for the process in front, and once the old window is gone the window under it is in front. `win32.c`'s `make_self()` comment (`:304-310`) records the same rule for a restart.

What asks for a recreation (the settings Mode row's rest, Discard, closing settings) is Task 1.9a. This task builds the recreation, runs it from the main loop, and gives Task 1.9a `window_follow_background()` to call.

**Why the focus handler filters by window ID (the spec leaves the choice to the plan).** Master's handler (`launcher.c:2522-2549`) reads `SDL_WINDOWEVENT_FOCUS_LOST` as an app's launch only while `state.application_launching`, and the main loop reads a return as `state.application_running && state.has_focus` (`launcher.c:2564`). A recreation never runs while an app is launching or running (it waits for `post_launch()`), so what it must not disturb is `state.has_focus` and, within 10 s of startup on Windows, the focus-lost branch that calls `set_foreground_window()` (`launcher.c:2536-2537`). The old window's focus-lost reaches SDL when Windows sends `WM_KILLFOCUS` as the new window takes the foreground, and SDL queues it; it can be read in the drain after the recreation's or a later one. A suppression flag would need an end it cannot know: cleared after one drain it can miss the event, held longer it swallows a real focus loss (an Alt+Tab). A filter on `event.window.windowID` against the window in force has no state and no end: every event of a replaced window carries that window's ID, whenever it arrives, and the new window's focus-gained is genuine and is read as it is today. It also drops the old window's pixel-size and display events, which the reload must not see. OpenGL's own `SDL_RecreateWindow` (Task 1.7) keeps the window's ID, so it passes the filter.

**Every holder of the window, and where it moves:**

| Holder | Where | Moved by |
|---|---|---|
| `window` (`SDL_Window *`) | `launcher.c:194` | `recreate_window()`, before the renderer is made |
| `renderer` (`SDL_Renderer *`), read through `extern` by `image.c:34`, `settings_fonts.c:19`, `settings_screen.c:30`, `settings_pickers.c:25` | `launcher.c:195` | `create_renderer()` on the new window; the old one is destroyed |
| `window_id` (new) | `launcher.c`, beside `vsync_on` (`:234`) | `recreate_window()` |
| `window_kind` (new) | same | `recreate_window()` |
| The HWND `win32.c` keeps (Task 1.2 replaced `wm_info`; `set_foreground_window()`, `start_process()`, `take_foreground()`, the hotkey calls) | `win32.c` | `platform_window_changed()` (Task 1.7) |
| The taskbar's mark | the taskbar | `platform_window_changed()` (Task 1.8a's addition) |
| Every texture (made on the old renderer) | everywhere Task 1.7's `drop_textures()` reaches | dropped, then rebuilt by the `RELOAD_RECREATE` reload |

Nothing outside `launcher.c` holds an `SDL_Window *` (`grep -rn "SDL_Window" src` on master finds only `launcher.c:194`). The cursor is hidden with SDL3's global `SDL_HideCursor()`, and text input is off on every new window by default, so neither needs moving.

- [ ] **Step 2: The new functions, after `raise_restarted_window()` (master `launcher.c:398-408`)**

```c
// A function to plan the window for a background on this platform, under SDL_RENDER_DRIVER when it is
// set. SDL_getenv, not getenv, which MSVC's /W4 /WX refuses as unsafe.
static WindowPlan plan_for(int background)
{
#ifdef _WIN32
    return window_plan(background, true, SDL_getenv("SDL_RENDER_DRIVER"));
#else
    return window_plan(background, false, SDL_getenv("SDL_RENDER_DRIVER"));
#endif
}

// A function to plan a new renderer for the window in force, after a lost device: the window's kind
// picks the list, never the Mode setting, which may have moved on while settings wait for a rest
static WindowPlan plan_in_force(void)
{
    return plan_for(window_kind == WINDOW_TRANSPARENT_SHORT ? BACKGROUND_TRANSPARENT : BACKGROUND_COLOR);
}

// A function to make the window a plan asks for, on a display, at native pixels: fullscreen (SDL3's
// desktop fullscreen), or for Transparent on Windows borderless and see-through at the display's
// top-left corner, one pixel row short of it. Shown, with no renderer yet; NULL, with SDL's reason,
// when it could not be made.
static SDL_Window *make_window(const WindowPlan *plan, SDL_DisplayID display)
{
    SDL_PropertiesID props = SDL_CreateProperties();
    if (props == 0)
        return NULL;
    SDL_SetStringProperty(props, SDL_PROP_WINDOW_CREATE_TITLE_STRING, PROJECT_NAME);
    SDL_SetBooleanProperty(props, SDL_PROP_WINDOW_CREATE_HIGH_PIXEL_DENSITY_BOOLEAN, true);
    if (plan->kind == WINDOW_TRANSPARENT_SHORT) {
        SDL_Rect bounds;
        if (!SDL_GetDisplayBounds(display, &bounds)) {
            SDL_DestroyProperties(props);
            return NULL;
        }
        WindowRect rect = window_short_rect((WindowRect) { bounds.x, bounds.y, bounds.w, bounds.h });
        SDL_SetBooleanProperty(props, SDL_PROP_WINDOW_CREATE_BORDERLESS_BOOLEAN, true);
        SDL_SetBooleanProperty(props, SDL_PROP_WINDOW_CREATE_TRANSPARENT_BOOLEAN, true);
        SDL_SetNumberProperty(props, SDL_PROP_WINDOW_CREATE_X_NUMBER, rect.x);
        SDL_SetNumberProperty(props, SDL_PROP_WINDOW_CREATE_Y_NUMBER, rect.y);
        SDL_SetNumberProperty(props, SDL_PROP_WINDOW_CREATE_WIDTH_NUMBER, rect.w);
        SDL_SetNumberProperty(props, SDL_PROP_WINDOW_CREATE_HEIGHT_NUMBER, rect.h);
    }
    else {
        // No size: a fullscreen window takes its display's bounds
        SDL_SetBooleanProperty(props, SDL_PROP_WINDOW_CREATE_FULLSCREEN_BOOLEAN, true);
        SDL_SetNumberProperty(props, SDL_PROP_WINDOW_CREATE_X_NUMBER, SDL_WINDOWPOS_UNDEFINED_DISPLAY(display));
        SDL_SetNumberProperty(props, SDL_PROP_WINDOW_CREATE_Y_NUMBER, SDL_WINDOWPOS_UNDEFINED_DISPLAY(display));
    }
    SDL_Window *made = SDL_CreateWindowWithProperties(props);
    SDL_DestroyProperties(props);
    return made;
}

// A function to say why no renderer could draw a transparent window, right after create_renderer()
// failed for its plan, while SDL's error is still that failure's
static void transparent_reason(const WindowPlan *plan, char *out, size_t size)
{
    if (plan->render_list != NULL)
        snprintf(out, size, "no renderer on the list %s could be made: %s", plan->render_list, SDL_GetError());
    else
        snprintf(out, size, "SDL_RENDER_DRIVER=%s could not be made: %s", SDL_getenv("SDL_RENDER_DRIVER"), SDL_GetError());
}

// A function to replace the window with one of the kind a plan gives, keeping StreamFlex in front, its
// hotkeys live and its focus logic quiet. The new window and its renderer are made while the old ones
// still stand: a process whose only window is gone loses its right to the foreground, and SDL drops
// pad events for an unfocused window. Every texture goes with the old renderer, every holder of the
// window moves to the new one, the new window takes the foreground while the old one still holds it,
// and only then does the old one go. The full reload is marked, whatever the new window's size. False,
// with SDL's reason, when the new window or its renderer could not be made: the old window, its
// renderer and its textures stand as they were.
static bool recreate_window(const WindowPlan *plan)
{
    SDL_Window *old_window = window;
    SDL_Renderer *old_renderer = renderer;
    SDL_DisplayID display = SDL_GetDisplayForWindow(old_window);
    SDL_Window *made = make_window(plan, display != 0 ? display : SDL_GetPrimaryDisplay());
    if (made == NULL)
        return false;
    window = made;
    renderer = NULL;
    if (!create_renderer(plan)) {
        char why[256];
        SDL_strlcpy(why, SDL_GetError(), sizeof(why));
        SDL_DestroyWindow(made);
        window = old_window;
        renderer = old_renderer;
        SDL_SetError("%s", why);   // The caller's reason: destroying the window may have set another error
        return false;
    }

    // Every texture belongs to the old renderer and goes before it; the reload makes them again
    drop_textures();
    if (old_renderer != NULL)
        SDL_DestroyRenderer(old_renderer);
    window_id = SDL_GetWindowID(window);
    window_kind = plan->kind;
#ifdef _WIN32
    platform_window_changed(old_window, window);   // The hotkeys leave the old HWND for the new, which is marked if Transparent
#endif

    // To the front while the old window still holds the foreground, which this process may then give
    SDL_RaiseWindow(window);
#ifdef _WIN32
    state.has_focus = take_foreground();
    if (state.has_focus)
        log_debug("Video: the new window is in front, with the keyboard");
    else
        log_error("Video: Windows kept the new window behind another, so it has no keyboard until it is brought to the front");
#endif
    SDL_DestroyWindow(old_window);
    reload_mark(RELOAD_RECREATE);
    return true;
}

// A function to give up on a transparent window: say why, show the Color background, and see that an
// opaque fullscreen window with a renderer is in force. A recreation that failed, or an override
// refused before it began, leaves the opaque window it would have replaced, still in front with its
// renderer and textures. A Transparent window whose renderer is gone (every device-loss attempt
// failed) is recreated opaque. At startup, with no window yet, the opaque one is made. Transparent is
// not tried again until another Mode is chosen and then Transparent again. Only an opaque window that
// gets no renderer ends StreamFlex.
static void fall_back_to_opaque(const char *reason)
{
    log_error("Video: no renderer can draw a transparent window (%s); using an opaque background", reason);
    transparent_failed = true;
    background_shown = BACKGROUND_COLOR;
    if (window != NULL && window_kind == WINDOW_FULLSCREEN && renderer != NULL) {
        set_draw_color();
        return;
    }
    WindowPlan plan = plan_for(BACKGROUND_COLOR);
    if (window != NULL) {
        if (!recreate_window(&plan))
            log_fatal("Could not initialize renderer\n%s", SDL_GetError());
        return;
    }
    window = make_window(&plan, SDL_GetPrimaryDisplay());
    if (window == NULL)
        log_fatal("Could not create SDL Window\n%s", SDL_GetError());
    window_id = SDL_GetWindowID(window);
    window_kind = plan.kind;
    if (!create_renderer(&plan))
        log_fatal("Could not initialize renderer\n%s", SDL_GetError());
}

// A function to give the window the kind the Mode setting needs. Closing settings, Discard and the
// Mode row's rest ask for it (Task 1.9a); the recreation itself runs in the main loop, between the
// drain and the reload, once no app is in front. An override outside the transparent list is refused
// here, by its name, before any renderer is made. On Linux every background is fullscreen, so this
// never asks for anything.
void window_follow_background(void)
{
    if (config.background_mode != BACKGROUND_TRANSPARENT)
        transparent_failed = false;   // Chosen again, Transparent is tried again
    WindowPlan plan = plan_for(config.background_mode);
    if (plan.refused) {
        if (!transparent_failed)
            fall_back_to_opaque(plan.reason);
        return;
    }
    recreate_wanted = window_must_change(window_kind, plan.kind, transparent_failed);
}

// A function to make the recreation window_follow_background() asked for, from the main loop after the
// drain and before the reload it marks. One asked for while an app is in front waits for post_launch().
static void run_recreation(void)
{
    recreate_wanted = false;
    WindowPlan plan = plan_for(transparent_failed ? BACKGROUND_COLOR : config.background_mode);
    if (plan.refused || plan.kind == window_kind)
        return;   // Asked for, then changed back before it ran
    if (plan.kind == WINDOW_TRANSPARENT_SHORT)
        log_debug("Video: recreating the window for a transparent background");
    else
        log_debug("Video: recreating the window for an opaque background");
    if (recreate_window(&plan))
        return;
    if (plan.kind != WINDOW_TRANSPARENT_SHORT)
        log_fatal("Could not initialize renderer\n%s", SDL_GetError());
    char why[512];
    transparent_reason(&plan, why, sizeof(why));
    fall_back_to_opaque(why);
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Startup makes the right kind of window directly (`create_window()`)**

After Tasks 1.2, 1.5 and 1.6, `create_window()` (master `launcher.c:357-393`) plans the window with `window_plan()`, makes it with `SDL_CreateWindow(..., SDL_WINDOW_FULLSCREEN | SDL_WINDOW_HIGH_PIXEL_DENSITY)`, `log_fatal()`s when that is NULL, hides the cursor, and `log_fatal()`s when `create_renderer(&plan)` returns false. Replace that run of statements, from the plan through the `create_renderer()` check, with the block below. What follows it in Task 1.6's version (`SDL_SyncWindow`, the geometry from the window's pixel size, Task 1.2's HWND read after the renderer) stays where it is, after this block. If Task 1.5's `create_renderer()` failure message reads differently from `"Could not initialize renderer\n%s"`, keep Task 1.5's text in all four `log_fatal()` calls of this task.

```c
    // The window the background needs, made as that kind from the start: Transparent on Windows is a
    // see-through window one row short of the display, every other background is fullscreen. An
    // override outside the transparent list is refused by its name, before any renderer is made; a
    // Transparent window that gets no renderer falls back to an opaque one.
    WindowPlan plan = plan_for(config.background_mode);
    if (plan.refused)
        fall_back_to_opaque(plan.reason);
    else {
        window = make_window(&plan, SDL_GetPrimaryDisplay());
        if (window == NULL)
            log_fatal("Could not create SDL Window\n%s", SDL_GetError());
        window_id = SDL_GetWindowID(window);
        window_kind = plan.kind;
        if (!create_renderer(&plan)) {
            if (plan.kind != WINDOW_TRANSPARENT_SHORT)
                log_fatal("Could not initialize renderer\n%s", SDL_GetError());
            char why[512];
            transparent_reason(&plan, why, sizeof(why));
            SDL_DestroyWindow(window);
            window = NULL;
            fall_back_to_opaque(why);
        }
    }
    SDL_HideCursor();
#ifdef _WIN32
    // Read from the window itself, after its renderer: OpenGL may have replaced the native window
    taskbar_mark_fullscreen(window, window_kind == WINDOW_TRANSPARENT_SHORT);
#endif
```

```c
// A function to test the count of failed presents on a Transparent window: the third in a row is a
// lost device, and the count starts again for the renderer that follows; a present that works ends a
// run, and an opaque window's failures are never counted
static void test_present_failures(void)
{
    int failures = 0;
    CHECK(!present_failed_lost(&failures, false, true));
    CHECK(!present_failed_lost(&failures, false, true));
    CHECK(present_failed_lost(&failures, false, true));
    CHECK_INT(failures, 0);
```

- [ ] **Step 4: The present counter (`src/winplan.h`, `src/winplan.c`)**

In `src/winplan.h`, after `window_must_change()`:

```c
// SDL_RenderPresent's failures in a row on a Transparent window that make a lost device: the Vulkan
// fix fails a swapchain the surface cannot give alpha whenever it is rebuilt (a resize, a VSync
// toggle, another display), and then every present fails with no render event (spec section 3)
#define PRESENT_FAILURES_LOST 3

// Counts one present into *failures; true once PRESENT_FAILURES_LOST in a row have failed on a
// Transparent window, and the count then starts again. A present that worked, or any present on
// another window, sets the count to 0.
bool present_failed_lost(int *failures, bool presented, bool transparent);
```

In `src/winplan.c`:

```c
// A function to count failed presents on a Transparent window, and say when they make a lost device
bool present_failed_lost(int *failures, bool presented, bool transparent)
{
    if (presented || !transparent) {
        *failures = 0;
        return false;
    }
    if (++*failures < PRESENT_FAILURES_LOST)
        return false;
    *failures = 0;
    return true;
}
```

```c
    static int present_failures = 0;   // SDL_RenderPresent's failures in a row on a Transparent window
    bool presented = SDL_RenderPresent(renderer);

    // Three in a row on a Transparent window are handled as SDL_EVENT_RENDER_DEVICE_LOST, which SDL
    // never sends for them: the next drain makes a new renderer from the Transparent list
    if (present_failed_lost(&present_failures, presented, window_kind == WINDOW_TRANSPARENT_SHORT)) {

        SDL_Event lost;
        SDL_zero(lost);
        lost.type = SDL_EVENT_RENDER_DEVICE_LOST;
        lost.render.windowID = window_id;
        if (!SDL_PushEvent(&lost))
            log_error("Renderer: could not treat the failing presents as a lost device\n%s", SDL_GetError());
    }
```

- [ ] **Step 6: The Transparent window follows its display (`launcher.c`)**

After `static void run_recreation(void);` in the forward declarations: `static void refit_transparent_window(void);`

After `run_recreation()`:

```c
// A function to fit a Transparent window to the display it is on again, one row short, after a
// display-mode change or a move to another display: SDL re-fits only a fullscreen window. No
// recreation: the SDL_EVENT_WINDOW_PIXEL_SIZE_CHANGED that follows runs the reload, and waits for an
// app in front as any size change does.
static void refit_transparent_window(void)
{
    if (window_kind != WINDOW_TRANSPARENT_SHORT)
        return;
    SDL_Rect bounds;
    SDL_DisplayID display = SDL_GetDisplayForWindow(window);
    if (display == 0 || !SDL_GetDisplayBounds(display, &bounds)) {
        log_error("Video: could not fit the transparent window to its display\n%s", SDL_GetError());
        return;
    }
    WindowRect rect = window_short_rect((WindowRect) { bounds.x, bounds.y, bounds.w, bounds.h });
    SDL_SetWindowPosition(window, rect.x, rect.y);
    SDL_SetWindowSize(window, rect.w, rect.h);
}
```

In the event drain's `switch`, a new case:

```c
                case SDL_EVENT_DISPLAY_CURRENT_MODE_CHANGED:
                case SDL_EVENT_DISPLAY_DESKTOP_MODE_CHANGED:
                    refit_transparent_window();
                    break;
```

and in Task 1.6's `case SDL_EVENT_WINDOW_DISPLAY_CHANGED:`, after its refresh-rate and `apply_frame_timing()` work and before its `break;`: `refit_transparent_window();`. If Task 1.6 already has cases for either display event, the call goes into them instead of a second case.

```c
// A function to move an image's opaque pixels off the chroma key (RGBA bytes), always, since
// Transparent mode can be chosen in settings after the image is loaded: on Linux a compositor's
// shader makes every pixel of the key color see-through. Windows' Transparent mode is per-pixel
// alpha, with no key, and ChromaKeyColor is ignored there, so nothing moves. `what` names the image
// for the log.
```

and make its body:


```c

#else
```

The `#else` form, not an early `return`, because MSVC's `/W4 /WX` fails code after an unconditional `return` (C4702, unreachable code). `UNUSED` is `util.h:17`'s, which `image.c` includes (`:15`).

